§E§H$EDICT_FUND$§!是一项单独的、静态的£unity£§Y凝聚力§!预算，用于支付§H法令§!的维护费。\n\n如果维护费超过了经费的规模，会使用常规的£unity£§Y凝聚力§!进行支付。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

§EThe §H$EDICT_FUND$§! is a separate and static pool of £unity£§YUnity§! for §HEdicts§! upkeep.\n\nIf upkeep exceeds the size of the Fund, regular £unity£§YUnity§! will also be used.§!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:50`

## envoy_events.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无礼的[envoy_source.GetAdj]特使

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

Insulting [envoy_source.GetAdj] Envoy

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:177`

## envoy_events.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎有第三方恶意入侵了驻在[Root.Capital.GetName]的[envoy_source.GetAdj]特使，[event_envoy.GetName]所使用的翻译软件，结果不同于平日宽宏的陈词滥调，[event_envoy.GetSheHe]开始恶毒地羞辱，把众人骂了个遍。[event_envoy.GetHerHis]设备的故障已被及时查明，但在此之前[Root.GetRulerTitle][Root.GetRulerName]已经被说成是“卑鄙的[ruler_species.GetNameInsult]”——他们肯定会对这种严重羞辱加以回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

It appears that a mischievous third party was able to hack into the translation software of the [envoy_source.GetAdj] envoy to [Root.Capital.GetName], [event_envoy.GetName], so that instead of uttering magnanimous platitudes, [event_envoy.GetSheHe] began spewing vicious insults that left none unscathed. The malfunction in [event_envoy.GetHerHis] equipment was duly uncovered, but not before [Root.GetRulerTitle] [Root.GetRulerName] was declared to be a "vile [ruler_species.GetNameInsult]" - a grave insult which surely calls for a response.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:178`

## envoy_events.1.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.1.A&lang=en)

### 中文

真是粗鲁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

How rude.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:179`

## envoy_events.1.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.1.B&lang=en)

### 中文

成吧，那还挺有趣的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

Well, that was funny!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:180`

## envoy_events.2.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.2.name&lang=en)

### 中文

翻译软件故障

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

Translation Software Malfunction

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:182`

## envoy_events.2.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.2.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎有第三方恶意入侵了我们派往[From.GetName]的特使[event_envoy.GetName]所使用的翻译软件，结果[event_envoy.GetHerHis]原本宽宏的外交辞令变成了恶毒的羞辱，把众人骂了个遍——尤其是他们的[From.GetRulerTitle]，被说成是“卑鄙的[ruler_species.GetNameInsult]”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

It appears that a mischievous third party was able to hack into the translation software of our envoy to the [From.GetName], [event_envoy.GetName], so that [event_envoy.GetHerHis] magnanimous platitudes instead became vicious insults that left none unscathed - not least their [From.GetRulerTitle], who was declared to be a "vile [ruler_species.GetNameInsult]".

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:183`

## envoy_events.2.desc.pos

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.2.desc.pos&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.2.desc.base$\n\n幸好，故障被迅速查明，[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]对这个事故报以幽默。不过“[Root.GetAdj]特使不当行为”的记录广为流传，在他国人民中被引为笑谈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

$envoy_events.2.desc.base$\n\nFortunately, the malfunction was swiftly uncovered and the [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural] took the incident with a sense of humor. Indeed, recordings of the "inappropriate [Root.GetAdj] envoy" are circulating widely, causing much mirth among their people.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:184`

## envoy_events.2.desc.neg

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.2.desc.neg&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.2.desc.base$\n\n尽管故障被迅速查明，[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]并不觉得这个事故好笑，并且正式控诉了我国特使的不当行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

$envoy_events.2.desc.base$\n\nAlthough the malfunction was swiftly uncovered, the [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural] were not amused at the incident and have lodged an official complaint at the inappropriate behavior of our envoy.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:185`

### English

A Contribution to Galactic Comedy

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:206`

## envoy_events.3.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.3.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

[event_envoy.GetName]，我们在星海共同体中代表我国利益的使者严重失态，把[council_country.GetAdj]代表错当成了[pathetic_country.GetSpeciesName]。不幸的是，许多旁观者听到了这条牵连甚广的评论，[event_envoy.GetName]如今自然饱受奚落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

[event_envoy.GetName], our emissary representing our interests in the Galactic Community, has committed a major faux pas, mistaking the [council_country.GetAdj] delegate for a [pathetic_country.GetSpeciesName]. Unfortunately, the incriminating remarks were witnessed by many onlookers, who duly showered [event_envoy.GetName] with ridicule.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:207`

### English

我们的一名特使严重失态，致使我国的外交使团变成了整个星系的笑料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

## envoy_events.4.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.4.name&lang=en)

### 中文

精干星系外交

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Astute Galactic Diplomacy

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:218`

## envoy_events.4.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.4.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们在星系中算不上真正的超级大国，我们却时常能在星系事务中发挥超过我国权重的实力。这在很大程度上归功于我们使者[event_envoy.GetName]的努力，这名特使在星海共同体中称职地代表了我国利益，连那些强硬反对[Root.GetName]立场的人们也对其高度尊敬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Although we are not able to count ourselves among the true superpowers of this galaxy, we have often been able to punch above our weight in galactic affairs. This is largely down to the exertions of our emissary [event_envoy.GetName], who ably represents our interests in the Galactic Community and commands deep respect even among those stridently opposed to all the [Root.GetName] stands for.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:219`

## envoy_events.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.4.desc.base$结果是，虽然不是每个人都畏惧我国海军或是渴望能有与我国相匹敌的金库，但他们依然景仰我国外交使团的高超造诣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

$envoy_events.4.desc.base$ As a result, even though not everyone may fear the might of our navy or long to match the depths of our coffers, they nevertheless admire the prowess of our diplomatic corps.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:220`

## envoy_events.4.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.4.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.4.desc.base$结果是，虽然不是每个人都畏惧我国海军或是渴望能有与我国相匹敌的物质资源，但他们依然景仰我国外交思维个体的高超造诣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

$envoy_events.4.desc.base$ As a result, even though not everyone may fear the might of our navy or long to match the extent of our material resources, they nevertheless admire the prowess of our diplomatic thought routines.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:221`

$envoy_events.4.desc.base$结果是，虽然不是每个人都畏惧我国海军或是渴望能有与我国相匹敌的物质资源，但他们依然景仰我国外交思维系统的高超造诣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

$envoy_events.4.desc.base$ As a result, even though not everyone may fear the might of our navy or long to match the extent of our material resources, they nevertheless admire the prowess of our diplomatic thought systems.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:222`

Diplomatic Corps Respected

## galactic_community_diplo_corps_respected_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_community_diplo_corps_respected_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我国的外交使团在全星系感性物种间广受尊敬。

### English

Our diplomatic corps are widely respected among the sentient species of the galaxy.

## envoy_events.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恼人的特使

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

A Most Irritating Envoy

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:226`

## envoy_events.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[envoy_source.GetAdj]特使[event_envoy.GetName]在[Root.Capital.GetName]上代表[envoy_source.GetAdj]利益的方式恼人又令人厌倦。自[event_envoy.GetHerHis]初来这里，[event_envoy.GetSheHe]就始终对[Root.GetGovernmentName]满怀敌意，对我国的政策和行动几乎持续不断地加以反对。[event_envoy.GetSheHe]实在太过烦人，如果能摆脱掉[event_envoy.GetHerHim]，没几个人会不开心的，而且我们已经收到传言，说一个[Root.GetAdj]忠诚者团体正阴谋加速[event_envoy.GetHerHis]的离世。\n\n允许这项阴谋继续下去当然十分容易，但让外国要人在我国境内被刺杀或许算不得什么好事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

The [envoy_source.GetAdj] envoy, [event_envoy.GetName], has been exasperatingly tiresome in [event_envoy.GetHerHis] efforts to represent [envoy_source.GetAdj] interests in [Root.Capital.GetName]. Right from [event_envoy.GetHerHis] first arrival, [event_envoy.GetSheHe] [event_envoy.GetHasHave] shown unremitting hostility towards the [Root.GetGovernmentName], unleashing an almost constant barrage of objections to our policies and actions. So great an irritation [event_envoy.GetHasHave] [event_envoy.GetSheHe] been that few would weep to see the back of [event_envoy.GetHerHim], and we have heard whispers that a group of [Root.GetAdj] loyalists are plotting to expedite [event_envoy.GetHerHis] demise.\n\nAllowing this scheme to succeed would certainly be convenient, but allowing foreign dignitaries to be assassinated in our realm might not be the best of looks.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:227`

## envoy_events.5.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.5.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能对此坐视不理。揪出这个阴谋！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

We will not stoop to such measures. Snuff out the plot!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:228`

## envoy_events.5.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.5.B&lang=en)

### 中文

就让这个烦人的特使消失好了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

Let us be rid of this troublesome envoy.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:229`

## envoy_events.6.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.6.name&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Capital.GetName]的谋杀！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Murder on [From.Capital.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:230`

## envoy_events.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[event_envoy.GetName]——我国派去调解与[From.GetName]间争议的特使被谋杀了！[event_envoy.GetSheHeCap]在几天前消失的无影无踪，错过了[event_envoy.GetHerHis]原计划与[Root.GetRulerTitle]议会进行的沟通。在搜索数日后，[event_envoy.GetHerHis]的遗体在一个即将被焚化的收纳箱中被发现。\n\n[From.GetAdj]政府推三阻四，不愿发起调查，让人怀疑他们是谋杀的幕后主使——尤其因为[event_envoy.GetName]近来对[From.GetGovernmentName]大加抨击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

[event_envoy.GetName] - the envoy we sent to mediate our disagreements with the [From.GetName] - has been murdered! [event_envoy.GetSheHeCap] disappeared without a trace several days ago, missing [event_envoy.GetHerHis] scheduled sync with the [Root.GetRulerTitle]'s council. After several days of searching, [event_envoy.GetHerHis] remains were found in a storage container destined to be incinerated.\n\nThe [From.GetAdj] authorities have been strangely evasive when asked to investigate, arousing suspicions that they were behind the killing - especially since [event_envoy.GetName] had been highly critical of the [From.GetGovernmentName] in recent times.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:231`

## kills_envoys_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akills_envoys_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于某种原因，外国特使在我国首都更容易猝然离世，这影响了我国在星海同侪中的地位。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

For some reason, foreign envoys' propensity to meet a sticky demise on our capital has affected our standing among our galactic peers.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:234`

## envoy_events.7.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.7.name&lang=en)

### 中文

亡命鸳鸯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

Star-Crossed Lovers

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:238`

## envoy_events.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我国特使[event_envoy.GetName]似乎并未完成[event_envoy.GetHerHis]的任务，好好维护[Root.GetAdj]利益，反而与一名[other_species_nation.GetAdj]外交官堕入爱河。由于担忧如此逾矩可能招致的恶果，这对恋人私奔去了[third_party.GetName]：那个国家危险的自由主义宪法允许这样的结合。\n\n如此一来，[event_envoy.GetName]已丧失了我国给予[event_envoy.GetHerHis]的特使职位。但是，对我们自己的官员失去掌控损害了我们的星际地位。我们必须逮捕这名不法之徒，恢复我们的名誉！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

It seems that, instead of performing [event_envoy.GetHerHis] task and representing [Root.GetAdj] interests, our envoy [event_envoy.GetName] has fallen for the charms of a [other_species_nation.GetAdj] diplomat. Fearing the repercussions of this transgression, the pair have eloped to the [third_party.GetName]: a nation whose dangerously liberal constitution permits such unions.\n\nIn doing this, [event_envoy.GetName] has forfeited [event_envoy.GetHerHis] position as our envoy. However, this loss of control over our own officials has hurt our galactic standing. We must restore our reputation by apprehending the miscreant!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:239`

## envoy_events.7.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.7.A&lang=en)

### 中文

[third_party.GetSpeciesNamePlural]欠我们人情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

The [third_party.GetSpeciesNamePlural] owe us their help.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:240`

## envoy_events.7.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.7.B&lang=en)

找到他们，向他们晓之以理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

Approach them and appeal to reason.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:241`

## envoy_events.7.B.tooltip

### English

If the [third_party.GetName] accepts, we will owe them 2 Favors.

## envoy_events.7.D

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.7.D&lang=en)

### 中文

随他们去吧；这件事很快就会过去的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

Let it be; the affair will be forgotten soon enough.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:243`

## envoy_events.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！我们注意到一名[From.GetAdj]罪犯正于贵国避难。这名不法之徒，亦即我国前特使[event_envoy.GetName]，背弃了一切效忠誓言，违反了我国最基本的法律，与一名[other_species_nation.GetAdj]私奔出逃。\n\n劳驾贵国将这名罪犯交还给我国，以便[event_envoy.GetSheHe]能因[event_envoy.GetHerHis]的罪行受到审判。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

Esteemed [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! It has come to our attention that a [From.GetAdj] criminal has taken refuge in your nation. The miscreant, our former envoy [event_envoy.GetName], broke all bonds of loyalty - along with the most fundamental laws of our nation - to elope with a [other_species_nation.GetAdj].\n\nWe would kindly ask that the criminal be returned to us, so that [event_envoy.GetSheHe] can face trial for [event_envoy.GetHerHis] crimes.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:244`

## envoy_events.8.A

当然可以。

### English

Of course.

### English

### English

## envoy_events.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

政治庇护于我们而言是神圣的。我方无法苟同你等的请求，交出那些没有做错任何事情的人们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Political asylum is sacred to us. We cannot countenance giving up those who have done no wrong to the likes of you!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:249`

## envoy_events.10.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.10.A&lang=en)

我方对此深表遗憾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

We are saddened to hear this.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:250`

### English

## envoy_events.11.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.11.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetAdj]特别行动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

[From.GetAdj] Special Ops Mission

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:255`

## envoy_events.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]似乎对我们拒绝向他们引渡其逃亡特使[event_envoy.GetName]非常不满，结果他们决定自己解决这个问题。这名逃亡者在我们眼皮子底下被绑架走了，似乎是一支训练有素的特别行动队在夜深人静时干了这件事。\n\n我们未能保护那些寻求我们帮助的人们，这在我们的盟友间引起了不小的关注，我们的外交官正在试着收拾局面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

It seems the [From.GetName] were so displeased by our refusal to extradite their rogue envoy [event_envoy.GetName] to them that they took matters into their own hands. The fugitive was stolen from under our noses at dead of night by what appears to have been a very well-drilled team of special operatives.\n\nOur inability to protect those who seek our aid has caused considerable concern among our allies, and our diplomats are scrambling to contain the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:256`

## envoy_events.11.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.11.A&lang=en)

### 中文

他们竟敢这样！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

How dare they!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:257`

## envoy_events.12.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.12.name&lang=en)

### 中文

特别行动成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

Special Ops Mission Successful

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:258`

## envoy_events.12.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.12.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派去抓捕逃亡特使[event_envoy.GetName]的小队成功完成任务。这名罪犯已被安全地收监，等待[event_envoy.GetHerHis]的罪行招致的重惩。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

The team we sent to retrieve our rogue envoy, [event_envoy.GetName], has successfully completed its mission. The criminal is now safely in our custody and faces a heavy punishment for [event_envoy.GetHerHis] sins.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:259`

## envoy_events.13.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.13.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetAdj]特别行动已被挫败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

[From.GetAdj] Special Ops Mission Thwarted

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:260`

## envoy_events.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]似乎对我们拒绝向他们引渡其逃亡特使[event_envoy.GetName]非常不满，结果他们打算自己解决这个问题，并发起了一项抓捕行动。不过，这项行动失败了，我们在[From.GetAdj]载有特别行动队员的舰船试图越过边境时将其拦截。同时我们也采取措施保证未来的企图也会以类似方式失败。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

It seems the [From.GetName] were so displeased by our refusal to extradite their rogue envoy [event_envoy.GetName] to them that they took matters into their own hands and launched a retrieval mission. However, this mission was bungled, as we intercepted the [From.GetAdj] vessel carrying their special operatives as it attempted to cross the border. Measures have been taken to ensure that future attempts will be similarly unsuccessful.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:261`

## envoy_events.13.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.13.A&lang=en)

### 中文

真不要脸……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

The cheek of it...

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:262`

## envoy_events.14.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.14.name&lang=en)

### 中文

特别行动失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

Special Ops Mission Unsuccessful

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:263`

## envoy_events.14.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.14.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派去抓捕逃亡特使[event_envoy.GetName]的小队未能完成任务；他们的舰船在试图越过边境时被抓到了[From.GetAdj]拘留点。[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]如预计中一般勃然大怒，抨击[Root.GetAdj]对他们事务的干涉。在他们看来，我国公民一定因我们对这件事的处理及抓捕罪犯失败而灰心丧气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

The team we sent to retrieve our rogue envoy, [event_envoy.GetName], was unsuccessful in its mission; their vessel was taken into [From.GetAdj] custody as they tried to cross the border. The [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are predictably incensed and are decrying [Root.GetAdj] interference in their affairs. For their part, our citizens are dismayed at our handling of the affair and our failure to retrieve the criminal.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:264`

Poor Guardians of Justice

## cannot_protect_asylum_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acannot_protect_asylum_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我国在同侪中的地位因我们未能保护已给予政治庇护的人们而有所下降。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Our standing among our peers is tarnished by our failure to protect those we offered political asylum.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:270`

## failed_special_ops_mission

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afailed_special_ops_mission&lang=en)

### English

Failed Special Ops Mission

## failed_special_ops_mission_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afailed_special_ops_mission_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们未能抓回逃亡特使，这既给我们的星系同侪敲响了警钟，又动摇了我国公民对政府的信心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Our failed attempt to retrieve a rogue envoy has both alarmed our galactic peers and shaken the faith of our citizens in our government.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:272`

## diplo_corps_lost_control

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adiplo_corps_lost_control&lang=en)

### 中文

外交使团受奚落

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

Diplomatic Corps Ridiculed

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:273`

## diplo_corps_lost_control_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adiplo_corps_lost_control_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在控制己方外交官时的无能不会激发同侪们的信心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

Our inability to keep control of our own diplomats does not inspire confidence among our peers.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:274`

## envoy_events.15.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.15.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇异生物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

A Strange Creature

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:276`

## envoy_events.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派往星海共同体的使者，[event_envoy.GetName]，最近花在照料一只跟在[event_envoy.GetHerHim]身边的奇异小型哺乳动物的时间多到令人不安。想必这是某位其他代表的家庭宠物，它可爱非常，但没有任何开智的迹象。尽管它十分喜好肉食，牙尖爪利，总体上却温柔喜人，希望以抚摸的形式大量进行身体上的接触——可它的脾气有时会变得暴烈。\n\n尽管这只生物已被证明会让[event_envoy.GetName]从更为严肃的任务中分心，其他代表却对[Root.GetSpeciesName]心灵中柔软、感性、惹人爱慕的一面多加赞誉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

[event_envoy.GetName], our emissary to the Galactic Community, has been spending a disturbing amount of time tending to a strange, small mammalian creature that began following [event_envoy.GetHerHim] around. Presumably a domestic pet that escaped from one of the other delegations, it is undeniably cute but shows no sign of sapient thought. Although it is highly carnivorous and exhibits sharp teeth and claws, it is generally affectionate and desires plentiful physical contact in the form of petting - though its mood swings can be sudden and violent!\n\nAlthough the creature has proved a distraction for [event_envoy.GetName]'s more serious tasks, other delegates have commented on the endearing nature of the softer, more sentimental side of the [Root.GetSpeciesName] psyche that has been revealed.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:277`

## envoy_events.15.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.15.A&lang=en)

### 中文

完美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

Perfect.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:278`

猫的爱恋

### English

Feline Affection

## envoy_events.17.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.name&lang=en)

### 中文

盟友间的争端

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

Disputes Among Allies

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:281`

## envoy_events.17.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]近来的法律修订引起了不小的骚动，我国与[dispute_winner.GetName]间旧有的意识形态争论重新被推上前台。我国代表，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetName]，就我方立场大胆地与[dispute_winner.GetAdj]行政官[dispute_winner_envoy.GetName]进行了辩论。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

The recent law changes in the [Root.Federation.GetName] caused a significant stir, with old ideological debates between ourselves and the [dispute_winner.GetName] once again rising to the fore. Our representative, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetName], bravely represented our side of the debate against the [dispute_winner.GetAdj] official, [dispute_winner_envoy.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:282`

## envoy_events.17.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetGovernmentName]各处的公民饶有兴致地观看了整场辩论，并对如何将自己的理想与[Root.Federation.GetName]的成员国资格相调和愈发担忧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

Citizens across the [Root.GetGovernmentName] watched the debate with interest, and are increasingly concerned about how to reconcile their ideals with membership in the [Root.Federation.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:283`

## envoy_events.17.desc.militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.militarist&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于军国主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，辩论逐渐对[dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHim]变得不利，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]看起来愈发紧张，在场各方都在向[dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHim]指责“[Root.GetAdj]穷兵黩武”。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more militaristic outlook, and as the debate turned against [dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHim], [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] appeared increasingly rattled as accusations of "[Root.GetAdj] warmongering" were made against [dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHim] from all sides.\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:284`

## envoy_events.17.desc.pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于和平主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，与会的其他人员大声叫嚷，斥责“[Root.GetAdj]的幼稚无知”威胁到了整个联邦的安全。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more pacifistic outlook, and faced vociferous retorts from all quarters that "[Root.GetAdj] naivety" allegedly threatens the safety of the entire federation.\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:285`

## envoy_events.17.desc.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于唯心主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，旁观者纷纷摇头反对，认为[Root.GetAdj]“迷信”十分“陈腐”。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more spiritualist outlook, and witnesses were left shaking their head at the "backwardness" of [Root.GetAdj] "superstitions".\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:286`

## envoy_events.17.desc.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于唯物主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，现场情势愈发紧张，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHis]的同僚不断谴责[Root.GetAdj]“享乐主义”，坚持认为他们的迷信观念十分优越。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more materialistic outlook, and grew increasingly rattled as [dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHis] peers continued to decry [Root.GetAdj] "hedonism", insisting on the superiority of their superstitious ways.\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:287`

## envoy_events.17.desc.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于亲外主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHis]的观点被断然谴责成“危险的自由主义”。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more xenophilic outlook, with [dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHis] outlook flatly condemned as "dangerously liberal".\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:288`

## envoy_events.17.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于排外主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHis]关于我们应当留意外来影响的郑重声明被说成是空洞、陈腐的偏见。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more xenophobic outlook, with [dispute_loser_envoy.GetHerHis] protestations that we should be wary of foreign influences decried as hollow and backwards prejudice.\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:289`

## envoy_events.17.desc.egalitarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.egalitarian&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于平等主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，那些生活在不太自由的社会中的人们看不到自由的好处，反而指责我们为“危险的自由主义”。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more egalitarian outlook, as those living in less free societies failed to see the benefits that greater liberties would give them, instead accusing us of being "dangerously liberal".\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:290`

## envoy_events.17.desc.authoritarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.17.desc.authoritarian&lang=en)

### 中文

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$可是，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe]关于威权主义益处的见解并未使其他在场人员信服，旁观者越来越觉得我们侵犯个人自由是一种罪恶。\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

$envoy_events.17.desc.base$ However, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetSheHe] [dispute_loser_envoy.GetWasWere] unable to convince those present of the benefits of a more authoritarian outlook, with witnesses increasingly convinced at the alleged evil of impinging on the liberties of the individual.\n\n$envoy_events.17.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:291`

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]近来的法律修订引起了不小的骚动，我国与[dispute_loser.GetName]间旧有的意识形态争论重新被推上前台。我国代表，[dispute_winner_envoy.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

The recent law changes in the [Root.Federation.GetName] caused a significant stir, with old ideological debates between ourselves and the [dispute_loser.GetName] once again rising to the fore. Our representative, [dispute_winner_envoy.GetName],

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:298`

## envoy_events.18.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

可是，[dispute_loser.GetSpeciesNamePlural]对这件事大为不满，抱怨其侵犯了他们的价值观念，并对联邦前进的方向表示担忧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

However, the [dispute_loser.GetSpeciesNamePlural] took this turn of events poorly, complaining at the encroachment on their values and expressing concern at the direction the federation is currently taking.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:299`

## envoy_events.18.desc.militarist.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.militarist.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了军国主义更有好处的见解，强调[dispute_loser.GetAdj]特使，[dispute_loser_envoy.GetName]的信条本质上懦弱而幼稚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

was able to persuade many of the benefits of a more militaristic outlook, highlighting the cowardice and naivety inherent in the beliefs of the [dispute_loser.GetAdj] envoy, [dispute_loser_envoy.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:300`

## envoy_events.18.desc.pacifist.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.pacifist.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了和平主义更有好处的见解，展示出[dispute_loser.GetAdj]穷兵黩武给我们所有人都带来了危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

was able to persuade many of the benefits of a more pacifistic outlook, demonstrating the dangers caused to us all by [dispute_loser.GetAdj] warmongering.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:301`

## envoy_events.18.desc.spiritualist.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.spiritualist.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了唯心主义更有好处的见解，向在场各方揭露了[dispute_loser.GetAdj]社会中因过度唯物而引发的问题，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

was able to persuade many of the benefits of spiritualism, revealing for all to see the problems excessive materialism causes [dispute_loser.GetAdj] society.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:302`

## envoy_events.18.desc.materialist.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.materialist.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了唯物主义更有好处的见解，展示了[dispute_loser.GetAdj]唯心迷信的陈腐之处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

was able to persuade many of the benefits of a materialist outlook, demonstrating the backwardness of [dispute_loser.GetAdj] spiritualist superstitions.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:303`

## envoy_events.18.desc.xenophile.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.xenophile.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了亲外主义更有好处的见解，在亲外的联邦中各物种相向而行，而非像[dispute_loser.GetAdj]特使[dispute_loser_envoy.GetName]所想要的那样长期互相猜忌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

was able to convince many of the benefits of a xenophilic outlook, where all species in the federation seek to get along, rather than constantly being suspicious of our peers, as the [dispute_loser.GetAdj] envoy [dispute_loser_envoy.GetName] would have us be.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:304`

## envoy_events.18.desc.xenophobe.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.xenophobe.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了排外主义更有好处的见解，在排外的联邦中各成员国都把自己的利益放在首位，而非如[dispute_loser.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的乌托邦理想主义一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

was able to convince many of the benefits of a xenophobic outlook, where federation members look out for themselves first, rather than following the utopian idealism of the [dispute_loser.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:305`

## envoy_events.18.desc.egalitarian.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.egalitarian.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了平等主义更有好处的见解，在平等主义下人人皆平等，而让[dispute_loser.GetSpeciesNamePlural]朝不保夕的威权主义则是邪恶的，安全与否全靠上位者脸色。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

was able to convince many of the benefits of a more egalitarian outlook, where all are considered free and evil, safe from the predations of authoritarianism that many [dispute_loser.GetSpeciesNamePlural] suffer under.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:306`

## envoy_events.18.desc.authoritarian.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenvoy_events.18.desc.authoritarian.base&lang=en)

### 中文

设法说服大多数人接受了威权主义更有好处的见解，在威权主义下法律与秩序得到遵行，我们可以远离[dispute_loser.GetAdj]关于平等的理想主义所引发的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

was able to convince many of the benefits of a more authoritarian outlook, with a focus on law and order that would allow us to escape the dangers of [dispute_loser.GetAdj] idealism about equality.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:307`

### English

Tiyanki Conservation Act

## resolution_tiyanki_conservation_act_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_tiyanki_conservation_act_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇是一种独特的太空生物，我们必须对其进行保护，使之免遭伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

The Tiyanki are a unique form of spacefaring life, and they must be protected from those who would do them harm.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:353`

## resolution_tiyanki_conservation_act_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_tiyanki_conservation_act_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

任何击杀§Y缇扬奇§!的帝国会被视为§R违反星系法§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Any empire that kills a §YTiyanki§! will be considered in §RBreach of Galactic Law§!.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:354`

## resolution_repeal_tiyanki_conservation_act

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_repeal_tiyanki_conservation_act&lang=en)

### 中文

§R废除$resolution_tiyanki_conservation_act$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

§RRepeal $resolution_tiyanki_conservation_act$§!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:355`

## resolution_repeal_tiyanki_conservation_act_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_repeal_tiyanki_conservation_act_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体不该决定其成员国如何对待缇扬奇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

It is not for the Galactic Community to decide how its members deal with the Tiyanki.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:356`

### English

## resolution_tiyanki_pest_control

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_tiyanki_pest_control&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇害兽管制

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

Tiyanki Pest Control

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:358`

## resolution_tiyanki_pest_control_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_tiyanki_pest_control_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些漫游的缇扬奇不只惹人厌烦，还实际危害了航行安全。绝不能容忍在星系中愈发繁忙的航道上有这种难以预测的生物存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

The roving Tiyanki are not only a nuisance, but also a navigational hazard. The presence of these unpredictable creatures cannot be tolerated in the galaxy's increasingly busy spacelanes.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:359`

## resolution_tiyanki_pest_control_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_tiyanki_pest_control_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

任何境内有§Y缇扬奇§!的帝国均处于§R违反星系法§!状态，直至这些生物被消灭或是自动离开为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Any empire with §YTiyanki§! inside their borders are in §RBreach of Galactic Law§! until the creatures are destroyed or leave of their own accord.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:360`

## resolution_repeal_tiyanki_pest_control

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_repeal_tiyanki_pest_control&lang=en)

### 中文

§R废除$resolution_tiyanki_pest_control$§!

## resolution_galacticreforms_motions_for_closure

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Motions for Closure

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:365`

## resolution_galacticreforms_motions_for_closure_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_galacticreforms_motions_for_closure_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当星海共同体在很大程度上达成一致时，在整个会议期间继续唇枪舌剑简直就是浪费时间。如果我们达成共识，那我们就应该尽早停止争论，继续讨论其他事宜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

When the Galactic Community is largely in agreement, it seems like a waste to continue debate for the full duration of the session. If we reach a consensus, we should end debate early and move on to other matters.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:366`

## resolution_galacticreforms_motions_for_closure_effect_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_galacticreforms_motions_for_closure_effect_success&lang=en)

### 中文

如果连续180天中，绝大多数（75%或以上）议员赞成或反对议案，参议院会议将提前结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Senate sessions will end early if there is a supermajority of 75% in favor or against the resolution for 180 consecutive days.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:367`

## resolution_galactic_market_repeal_sentient_organic_slave_trade_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_galactic_market_repeal_sentient_organic_slave_trade_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经济指标证明，我们先前对有机体奴隶贸易的限制显然大错特错。我们应当废除禁令，看自由市场如何化腐朽为神奇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

Economic indicators prove that our earlier restrictions on the organic slave trade were clearly in error. We should repeal the ban and let the free market do its magic.

## resolution_galactic_market_ban_sentient_slave_trade_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_galactic_market_ban_sentient_slave_trade_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

遗憾的是，我们先前的议案有漏洞，导致有人买卖有情感的机械体或有机体。我们应该亡羊补牢，禁止在泛星系贸易市场上交易任何感性生命体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

It is regrettable that our previous resolution left a loophole for the sale of sentient mechanical organisms. We should rectify this mistake and ban the trade of all sentients on the Galactic Market.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:382`

### 中文

创造者应该有权随意处置其所有物，包括能模仿情感的物体。我们应该补偏救弊，废除所谓“有情感的”机器的交易禁令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

Creators should have the right to do what they wish with their property. This includes objects that imitate sentience. We should repeal the misguided ban on the sale of these so-called 'sentient' machines.

### 中文

我们可以容忍没有灵魂的机械存在，但前提是我们能从中获益。我们应该对他们的生产征税，并利用所得行大义之事。

机械帝国和经历过合成进化的机械体的£diplo_weight£§Y$diplomatic_weight$§!减少§R-25%§!。\n\n§Y$SYNTHETIC_EVOLUTION_PROJECT$§!特殊项目现已§R禁用§!。

### English

## resolution_divinity_a_defined_purpose

神裁使命

### English

A Defined Purpose

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:410`

## resolution_divinity_a_defined_purpose_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_divinity_a_defined_purpose_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

机械体是工具，必须更好地服务于人。我们应该颁布法律，确保这些没有灵魂的人口永远臣服于其形而上的既定主人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Machines are tools and must serve their betters. We should enact legislation to ensure that the spiritually bereft are forever in servitude to their metaphysically endowed superiors.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:411`

## resolution_divinity_a_defined_purpose_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_divinity_a_defined_purpose_effect&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

The §Y$ai_full_rights_name$§! $policy_artificial_intelligence_policy$ policy is §Rbanned§!.\n\n§Y$auth_machine_intelligence$§! empires that are not §Y$personality_servitors$§! are in §RBreach of Galactic Law§!, but will be given the option to embrace the civic.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:412`

## federations.10340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations.10340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体已通过§Y$resolution_divinity_a_defined_purpose$§!议案，禁用§Y$ai_full_rights_name$§!$policy_artificial_intelligence_policy$政策和非§Y失控机仆§!机械智能。\n\n我们的帝国目前不符合此议案的规定，要遵守此议案，更改相关政策吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

The Galactic Community has passed the §Y$resolution_divinity_a_defined_purpose$§! resolution, which bans the §Y$ai_full_rights_name$§! $policy_artificial_intelligence_policy$ policy and non-§YRogue Servitor§! Machine Intelligences.\n\nOur Empire is currently in breach of this resolution. Shall we change our policies to be in compliance?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:434`

### 中文

$resolution_tiyanki_pest_control$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

$resolution_tiyanki_pest_control$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:445`

## galcom.104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇已被宣布为星系害兽。任何境内有缇扬奇的星海共同体成员国都有法律义务确保这些生物被即刻消灭。\n\n不幸的是，我国境内就有缇扬奇存在。直至它们被处理掉或是自动离开我国境内为止，[Root.GetName]将被视为§R违反星系法§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

The Tiyanki have officially been declared galactic pests. Any member of the Galactic Community with Tiyanki inside their borders are legally obliged to see to the immediate euthanization of the creatures.\n\nUnfortunately, there are Tiyanki present within our space. Until they are dealt with, or they leave our space of their own accord, the [Root.GetName] will be considered in §RBreach of Galactic Law§!.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:446`

## galcom.106.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.106.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇进入[Root.GetSpeciesName]领土

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Tiyanki In [Root.GetSpeciesName] Space

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:447`

## galcom.106.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.106.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一只缇扬奇越过边境，进入了我国的§H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§!星系。由于缇扬奇已被宣布为星系害兽，任何境内有缇扬奇的星海共同体成员国都有法律义务确保这些生物被即刻消灭。\n\n[Root.GetName]将被视为§R违反星系法§!，直至缇扬奇被处理掉或是自动离开我国境内为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

A Tiyanki pod has crossed into our space in the §H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§! system. As the Tiyanki have officially been declared galactic pests, all Galactic Community members are legally obliged to immediately euthanize any creatures present within their borders.\n\nThe [Root.GetName] will be considered in §RBreach of Galactic Law§! until the Tiyanki have either departed our space or been exterminated.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:448`

## galcom.107.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.107.name&lang=en)

### 中文

违反缇扬奇保护法

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

Tiyanki Conservation Act Violation

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:452`

## galcom.107.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.107.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体的官员就我们最近在§H[tiyanki_kill_system.GetName]§!星系击杀缇扬奇提出抗议，通知我们我国的行为违反了缇扬奇保护法。结果是，[Root.GetName]将在接下来10年内被视为§R违反星系法§!。\n\n我们从缇扬奇尸体上提取的资源被收缴了，而且他们还对我国处以罚金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

Officials from the Galactic Community have protested our recent killing of Tiyanki in the §H[tiyanki_kill_system.GetName]§! system, informing us that our actions have violated the Tiyanki Conservation Act. As a consequence, the [Root.GetName] is to be considered in §RBreach of Galactic Law§! for the next ten years.\n\nThe resources we extracted from the dead Tiyanki have been confiscated, and we have been issued a fine.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:453`

## galcom.107.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.107.a&lang=en)

### 中文

太过分了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

This is an outrage!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:454`

## galcom.109.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.109.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体的官员注意到[Root.GetName]境内有缇扬奇存在。由于缇扬奇害兽管制议案已经生效，这使得我国处于§R违反星系法§!状态，直至这些生物被消灭或是自行主动离开为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

Officials from the Galactic Community have observed the presence of Tiyanki within the borders of the [Root.GetName]. With the Tiyanki Pest Control resolution having been enacted, this leaves us in §RBreach of Galactic Law§! until the creatures are euthanized or leave our space of their own accord.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:455`

## resolution_amoeba_conservation_act

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_amoeba_conservation_act&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫保护法案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

Space Amoeba Protection Act

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:457`

## resolution_amoeba_conservation_act_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_amoeba_conservation_act_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管外表平平无奇，但与其他所有种群一样，太空变形虫也有在星系中畅游的权利。我们必须将其视为值得珍视的一种生命形态，而非可以随意捕杀的害虫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Despite their unseeming looks, the Space Amoebas have as much right to roam the galaxy as any species. They must be treated as a form of life worth cherishing, not as vermin to be hunted down.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:458`

## resolution_amoeba_conservation_act_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_amoeba_conservation_act_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

Any empire that does not agree to peacefully coexist with §YSpace Amoebas§! will be considered in §RBreach of Galactic Law§!.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:459`

## galactic_features.505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫保护法案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

The Space Amoeba Protection Act

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:463`

## galactic_features.505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体已通过§Y$resolution_amoeba_conservation_act$§!议案，要求帝国与§Y太空变形虫§!和平共处。\n\n我们的帝国目前不符合此议案的规定，要遵守此议案，更改相关政策吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

The Galactic Community has passed the §Y$resolution_amoeba_conservation_act$§! resolution, mandating that empires peacefully coexist with §YSpace Amoebas§!.\n\nOur Empire is currently in breach of this resolution. Shall we change our policies to be in compliance?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:464`

## federations.10400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations.10400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体的特使们撰写了一封措辞强烈的文章，声称[Root.GetName]违反了星系法，并警告我们不遵守星系法可能带来的严重后果。\n\n我们是否要改变自己的政策，以迎合这一要求？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

Envoys from the Galactic Community have compiled a list of articles that the [Root.GetName] are in violation of, and warn of possible consequences should we not make ourselves in alignment with Galactic Law.\n\nShould we change our policies and accede to their demands?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:483`

## galactic_features.367.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.367.1&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§!中的最后一只缇扬奇已被§H[tiyanki_killer.GetName]§!击杀。由于它们的古老繁殖地已被净化，缇扬奇再也不能继续繁殖了，这是星系历史上的第一次。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

The last of the Tiyanki in the §H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§! system have been exterminated by the §H[tiyanki_killer.GetName]§!. With their ancient breeding grounds scoured, the Tiyanki will no longer be able to reproduce for the first time in galactic history.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:508`

## galactic_features.367.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.367.2&lang=en)

### 中文

它们走向最终灭绝似乎无可避免。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Their path to eventual extinction seems unavoidable now.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:509`

## galactic_features.367.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.367.3&lang=en)

### 中文

由于[tiyanki_system.GetName]外的全部已知缇扬奇已被捕杀，今天就标志着缇扬奇物种的终结。虽然缇扬奇曾在星系中叱咤了千百万年，但它们现在已经灭绝了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

With all known Tiyanki outside of [tiyanki_system.GetName] having already been hunted down, today marks the end of their species. After having roamed the galaxy for hundreds of millions of years, the Tiyanki are now extinct.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:510`

## galactic_features.367.4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.367.4&lang=en)

### 中文

有传闻称尚有一只古老的缇扬奇族母逍遥在外。不论是否为真，从今天起，缇扬奇作为一个物种只能被看作已经灭绝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

There are rumored sightings of an ancient Tiyanki Matriarch that yet remains at large. Whether true or not, after today the Tiyanki - as a species - can only be considered extinct.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:511`

## galactic_features.368.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.368.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生命摇篮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

Cradle of Life

## galactic_features.368.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.368.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§!主序星旁，四颗于低轨道环绕的气态巨行星被笼罩在等离子场中。充能的等离子场深入气态巨行星大气内部，这种独特的环境是许多太空生物繁殖的温床。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

Four gas giants enshrouded by a vast plasma field share a close orbit around the §H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§! system's primary. The charged plasma field extends well into the atmospheres of these gas giants, and this unique environment serves as a breeding ground for a large number of spacefaring organisms.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:515`

## galactic_features.368.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.368.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些生物大都微乎其微，但有一个显著的例外——巨型§H缇扬奇§!。这个恒星系一定就是它们起初进化了千百万年的地方，随后它们开始在整个星系中漫游。由于这个恒星系的等离子场性质极为罕见，这里很可能是星系中缇扬奇唯一的繁殖地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

These organisms are all miniscule, with one notable exception - the massive §HTiyanki§!. This system must be where they originally evolved hundreds of millions of years ago, before they ranged deeper into the galaxy. Given the extremely rare properties of the system's plasma field, this is likely the only place in the galaxy where the Tiyanki can reproduce.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:516`

## galactic_features.368.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.368.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些生物大都微乎其微，但有一个显著的例外——一种我们先前从未见过的大型生物。尽管我们对它们掌握的数据并不多，但是它们似乎会非常频繁地在星系中游荡。如果我们对它们能够了解更多，我们或许能够发觉是什么吸引着它们前来这个恒星系，以及这一现象是否能够给我们带来益处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

These organisms are all miniscule, with one notable exception - massive creatures unlike anything we have ever seen. Although we possess little data on them, they seem to frequently range deep into the galaxy. If we learn more about them we might figure out what attracts them to this system, and if it can be beneficial to us in any way.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:517`

## galactic_features.370.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.370.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最后一只已知缇扬奇已经在§H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§!星系中被§H[tiyanki_killer.GetName]§!捕杀。这些太空生物出现的时间比所有现存帝国更早，据信已经在星系中叱咤了千百万年。今天就是它们故事的终结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

The last known Tiyanki was recently hunted down and killed by the §H[tiyanki_killer.GetName]§! in the §H[tiyanki_system.GetName]§! system. These spacefaring life forms, who were far older than any currently existing empire, are believed to have roamed the galaxy for hundreds of millions of years. Today marks the end of their storied history.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:520`

## galactic_features.371.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.371.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很久很久以前，即使在我们国家还如日中天的时候，缇扬奇已经被视作古老的生物了……现在我听说一批[Root.GetSpeciesName]舰船最近屠杀了这些手无寸铁的生物，使得缇扬奇彻底灭绝。\n\n你们卑微的[Root.GetSpeciesName]大脑能理解自己干的事情吗？你们对自己无情的举动从星系中窃取走的东西有最起码的理解吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

Even when our empire was at the height of its power, eons ago, the Tiyanki were considered ancient, and now... now I am told that a bunch of [Root.GetSpeciesName] WHELPS recently slew the last of the defenseless creatures, rendering them extinct.\n\nCan your feeble [Root.GetSpeciesName] brains comprehend what you have done? Do you have even the slightest notion of what your callous actions have stolen from this galaxy?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:521`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.371.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

## galactic_features.385.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.385.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[tiyanki_kill_system.GetName]§!星系中成功捕杀了一些野生缇扬奇。我方舰队用激光切割以从巨型缇扬奇的尸体内提取矿物和资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

We have successfully hunted down and killed some wild Tiyanki in the §H[tiyanki_kill_system.GetName]§! system. Our fleet has used laser cutters to extract materials and resources from the massive Tiyanki remains.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:529`

## galcom.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalcom.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体的官员收到了我们最近就缇扬奇族母取得胜利的消息。他们告诉我方，消灭这只野兽明显违反了缇扬奇保护法，就算缇扬奇族母是敌对生物也一样。\n\n[Root.GetName]将在接下来十年内被视为§R违反星系法§!。此外，我们收到了一份巨额的罚单，理由是我们消灭了“一种独特的，整个星系中再也见不到的缇扬奇生命形式”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

Officials from the Galactic Community have received word of our recent victory against the Tiyanki Matriarch. They have informed us that our destruction of the beast was a clear violation of the Tiyanki Conservation Act, regardless of the Matriarch's hostile nature.\n\nThe [Root.GetName] will be considered in §RBreach of Galactic Law§! for the next ten years. Additionally, we have been issued with a hefty fine for our killing of "a unique Tiyanki life form whose like the galaxy shall never see again."

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:538`

## 27_HOSTILE_GREETING

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3A27_HOSTILE_GREETING&lang=en)

### 中文

你们对缇扬奇的野蛮攻击是不可饶恕的罪行。总有一天，清算会到来的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

Your savage attacks on the Tiyanki are unforgivable crimes. One day, there shall be a reckoning.

## 25_NEUTRAL_GREETING

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3A25_NEUTRAL_GREETING&lang=en)

### 中文

你们肆意残杀手无寸铁的缇扬奇，这不会被忽略的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

Your wanton murder of defenseless Tiyanki has not gone unnoticed.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:548`

## 25_FRIENDLY_GREETING

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3A25_FRIENDLY_GREETING&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇从来算不上威胁……你们是出于什么而攻击缇扬奇的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

The Tiyanki have never been a threat... what could possibly have compelled you to attack them?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:549`

## tiyanki_graveyard_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiyanki_graveyard_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇坟场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

Tiyanki Graveyard

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:555`

## tiyanki_graveyard_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiyanki_graveyard_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§!恒星系发现的一颗完全被缇扬奇尸体覆盖了的行星。想研究这一怪异的埋葬地，我们必须先解决它的守墓人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

A planet entirely covered in the bodies of dead Tiyanki has been found in the §H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§! system. In order to study this unusual burial ground, we must first deal with its gravekeepers.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:556`

## TIYANKI_LURE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATIYANKI_LURE&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇诱饵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

Tiyanki Lure

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:559`

## TIYANKI_LURE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATIYANKI_LURE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇以气体为食。建造一座气体散播站，我们就能引走那些守在§H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§!恒星系的缇扬奇，并且接近他们的坟场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

Tiyanki feed on gas. By building a gas diffuser station, we may lure out the Tiyanki stationed in the §H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§! system and gain access to their burial ground.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:560`

这座空间站释放富有营养的气体云，把守墓缇扬奇勾引离开值守。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

The station releases clouds of nourishing gas, diverting the Tiyanki gravekeepers from their duties.

肃清守墓缇扬奇

Eliminate the Tiyanki Gravekeepers

## tiyanki_graveyard_poi_desc

### 中文

肃清守在§H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§!的缇扬奇以接近他们的坟场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Eliminate the Tiyanki stationed in the §H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§! to gain access to their burial ground.
