## galactic_features.387.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.387.name&lang=en)

### 中文

守墓兽群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

Gravekeepers

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:571`

## galactic_features.387.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.387.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生活在§H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§!星系的缇扬奇的行为与该物种的已知行为模式并不一致。缇扬奇通常是迁徙性的，而这群缇扬奇却紧紧守在一颗满是他们同类尸体的行星周围。\n\n它们遮天蔽日的触手阻碍了对这个坟地的研究，除非我们将它们引走或者处置掉，否则进一步的调查难以进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

The actions of the Tiyanki inhabiting the §H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§! system do not match known behavior patterns for their species. While Tiyanki are usually nomadic, these herds remain stationed around a planet entirely covered in the carcasses of their own dead.\n\nAttempts to study the burial ground have been hindered by the net of tentacles the Tiyanki have wrapped around the planet, making further research impossible - unless we are willing to displace or dispose of the gravekeepers.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:572`

## galactic_features.387.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.387.a&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇就还挺弱的。宰了它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

Tiyanki make easy prey. Eliminate them.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:573`

## galactic_features.387.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.387.b&lang=en)

### 中文

在不伤害他们的前提下引走吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

Displace the gravekeepers without harming them.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:574`

## galactic_features.388.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.388.name&lang=en)

### 中文

亵渎墓地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Desecrated Grave

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:577`

## galactic_features.388.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.388.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在肃清停留在[FromFrom.System.GetName]的缇扬奇之后，我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]可以安全地接近墓地，进一步开展研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

Having eliminated the Tiyanki stationed in [FromFrom.System.GetName], our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] can now safely approach the burial ground to conduct their research.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:578`

## galactic_features.389.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.389.name&lang=en)

### 中文

可口的诱饵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

Tasty Bait

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:581`

## galactic_features.389.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.389.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经在§H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§!星系完成了缇扬奇诱饵的建设。受到散播装置释放的营养性气体的误导，守墓的兽群被诱惑离开了他们的驻地，允许我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]悄悄溜过它们的触手，在不引起注意的情况下接近墓地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

The construction of the Tiyanki lure in the §H[tiyanki_graveyard_system.GetName]§! system has been completed. Beguiled by the nourishing gases released by the diffuser, the gravekeepers are lured away from their post, allowing our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] to slip through their tentacles and approach the burial ground unnoticed.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:582`

## galactic_features.384.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.384.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘的守护者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

Mysterious Guardians

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:585`

## galactic_features.384.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.384.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一大群§H[tiyanki_no_contact.GetName]§!聚集在一个行星的周边，上面遍布某种外星生命体的尸体。\n\n由于我们对于这些外星实体没有任何信息，我们只能对它们的行为进行猜测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

A large number of §H[tiyanki_no_contact.GetName]§! are clustered around a planet covered in the carcasses of some alien life-form.\n\nHaving no information on those alien entities, we can only speculate on their behavior.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:586`

## galactic_features.384.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.384.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

同§H[tiyanki_no_contact.GetName]§!建立第一次接触以了解更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

Establish first contact with the §H[tiyanki_no_contact.GetName]§! to learn more.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:587`

## TIYANKI_ASTEROID_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATIYANKI_ASTEROID_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

活体小行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

Living Asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:590`

## TIYANKI_ASTEROID_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATIYANKI_ASTEROID_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

从小行星带取回的岩石样本包含大量的生物组织，这让人始料未及。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Rock samples retrieved from the asteroid belt unexpectedly contain a large amount of biological tissue.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:591`

## anomaly.7100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

打开墓地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

Open Burial

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:594`

## anomaly.7100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]一开始以为这是小行星，结果发现这其实是冰冻的、被压实的缇扬奇尸体，在轨道上历经几十年之后，成为一堆不成形状的块状物。整个小行星环，实际上，是一片露天墓地。其中的一部分尸体是最近刚刚到达并开始腐坏的，而剩下的已历经千年岁月，甚至比我们的帝国还要久远。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

What our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] first mistook for asteroids have been revealed to be frozen, battered Tiyanki corpses reduced to shapeless lumps after decades in orbit. The entire asteroid ring is, in fact, just an open burial ground. Some of the bodies we acquired are recently deceased, while others predate our empire by thousand of years.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:595`

## anomaly.7100.alt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7100.alt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]一开始以为这是小行星，结果发现这其实是冰冻的、被压实的外星动物尸体，其体型基本和在这个恒星系游荡的外星生命体一致。整个小行星环，实际上，是一片露天墓地。其中的一部分尸体是最近刚刚到达并开始腐坏的，而剩下的已历经千年岁月，甚至比我们的帝国还要久远。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

What our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] first mistook for asteroids have been revealed to be frozen, battered corpses of alien fauna, roughly matching the shape of the aliens seen roaming the system. The entire asteroid ring is, in fact, just an open burial ground. Some of the bodies we acquired are recently deceased, while others predate our empire by thousand of years.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:596`

## site_tiyanki_graveyard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_tiyanki_graveyard&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇乱坟岗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

Tiyanki Grave Mound

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:599`

## site_tiyanki_graveyard_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_tiyanki_graveyard_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

整个[From.Planet.GetName]的地表遍布死亡缇扬奇的遗骸。我们的传感器没有检测到生命的信号，然而这些尸体仍然在不停地蠕动，仿佛有什么看不见的力在驱动这些尸体一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

The surface of [From.Planet.GetName] is entirely covered in the remains of dead Tiyanki. Our sensors detect no signs of life, and yet the corpses still writhe and squirm, as if animated by some inscrutable force.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:600`

## ancrel.14100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

肉之球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

Meatball

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:602`

## ancrel.14100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们实在是很难说[From.Planet.GetName]到底算不算作一个行星：初步钻探发现的是一层又一层的生物组织。据估计，总共需要2,435,463只缇扬奇尸体才能产生此等规模的行星质量（误差范围5.3%）。即使我们假设地核是一块岩石，尸体只是堆叠在上面，涉及到的样本数量依然是无比庞大的。\n\n是某人或者某种事物将缇扬奇带到这里让它们死去，还是说它们只是顺其自然地来到这里度过最后的时光，然后死去？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

It's hard to determine if [From.Planet.GetName] is even a planet: preliminary drilling has only revealed strata upon strata of biological tissue. According to estimations, it would take 2,435,463 Tiyanki bodies to produce a planetary mass of this size (with a margin of error equal to 5.3%). Even assuming the existence of a rocky core upon which the corpses are layered, the number of specimens involved is still significant.\n\nDid someone or something bring the Tiyanki here to die, or are they naturally drawn to this place as their final hour draws near?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:603`

## ancrel.14100.necro.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14100.necro.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.14100.desc$最重要的是：为什么我们的死灵法师不能复活它们？为什么这堆死去的肉体拒绝我们，就好像已经有其他存在在控制它们一样？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

$ancrel.14100.desc$ Most crucially: why are our Necromancers unable to revive them? Why does this dead flesh reject us, as if already controlled by another entity?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:604`

## ancrel.14200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

死亡三步

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Three Parts Dead

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:606`

## ancrel.14200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

遍布[From.Planet.GetName]地表的死亡缇扬奇的那种不寻常……的活动，似乎是它们再生能力的一种副作用：即使技术上已经判定为死亡，它们的尸体仍旧在不断地重组再生。这个恒星系拥有着非比寻常般丰富的营养物质，尤其是缇扬奇游荡的那个气态巨行星。或许这解释了为什么我们之前从未观察到这样的行为。\n\n然而，这个发现并没有解释为什么这些死去的生物像是在被某种单一思维控制一样蠕动，不断地伸出触手，吞食新近降落到这个行星上的尸体。\n\n我们希望进一步的挖掘能够揭示这个地方，或这个生命……的内在运作规律，带给我们新的见解。我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]还在就术语和分类问题无法达成一致。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

The unusual... liveliness of the dead Tiyanki covering the surface of [From.Planet.GetName] seems to be a byproduct of their regenerative capabilities: even if technically dead, the bodies are still reassembling themselves. This system is unusually rich in nourishing substances typically found in the gas giants where Tiyanki graze. Perhaps that explains why this behavior hasn't been observed before.\n\nThis discovery does not explain why the dead seem to move as if controlled by a single mind, extending their tentacles to encroach upon the new carcasses falling on the planet.\n\nFurther excavations will hopefully reveal new insights on the inner workings of this... place. Or being. Our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] are still divided on the exact nomenclature.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:607`

## ancrel.14300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

尸山血河迷宫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

Flesh Labyrinth

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:609`

## ancrel.14300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的挖掘队发掘到了一个复杂的隧道网络，从[From.Planet.GetName]的地表一直延伸到地下深处。化学分析确认迷宫的墙体是由压实的缇扬奇组织构成，尽管其年代甚至超过了我们的帝国，但肉质还散发着温热，甚至几乎没有腐败。看上去就像是这些尸体自己聚合到了一起，重构其形态建立了这个地方……但是为什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Our excavations unearthed a complex network of tunnels extending under the surface of [From.Planet.GetName]. Chemical analysis confirms the walls are entirely composed of highly compressed Tiyanki tissue, the flesh still warm and hardly decomposed despite rivaling the age of our empire. It's as if the corpses were fused together, reshaped and modified to form this place... But why?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:610`

## ancrel.14400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隧道魅影

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

Haunted Tunnels

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:612`

## ancrel.14400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对[From.Planet.GetName]的地下隧道网络进行测绘的时候，我们[Owner.GetScientistPlural]突然意识到，这个隧道网络同一只缇扬奇的血管系统极其相似——但是其规模达到了行星级别。\n\n那些搏动的墙壁散发着轻微的温暖，随着探险队逐渐接近行星的核心，墙壁的温度也在慢慢提升——在核心处，我们的扫描仪捕捉到了一个微弱的生命信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

After mapping the extensive underground network of [From.Planet.GetName], our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] realized the tunnels closely match the structure of the Tiyanki vascular system - but on a planetary scale.\n\nThe pulsating walls emanate a soft warmth, the temperature slowly increasing as the expedition approached the planet core - where our scanners have picked up a faint life signal.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:613`

## ancrel.14400.matriarch.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14400.matriarch.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对[From.Planet.GetName]的地下隧道网络进行测绘的时候，我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]突然意识到，这个隧道网络同一只缇扬奇的血管系统极其相似——但是其规模跟我们之前遇到的那只在星辰间游荡的缇扬奇族母差不多。\n\n那些搏动的墙壁散发着轻微的温暖，随着探险队逐渐接近行星的核心，墙壁的温度也在慢慢提升——在核心处，我们的扫描仪捕捉到了一个微弱的生命信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

After mapping the extensive underground network of [From.Planet.GetName], our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] realized the tunnels closely match the structure of the Tiyanki vascular system - but on a scale comparable to that of the Tiyanki Matriarch we encountered while roaming the stars.\n\nThe pulsating walls emanate a soft warmth, the temperature slowly increasing as the expedition approached the planet core - where our scanners have picked up a faint life signal.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:614`

## ancrel.14510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

主脑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Mother Brain

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:616`

## ancrel.14510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个庞大的地下穴室中，一只缇扬奇竖立地站着，钟型的脑袋微微地摇晃着，它细线一样的触手舒展开来，连接着穴室的外墙。看上去它成为了某种控制单元，不断操纵、指引着这个行星级别墓地的生长。\n\n我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]没法断定什么种类的生物会从如此恐怖的过程中诞生，但是当它苏醒的时候，有一件事是很明确的：它会成为远比普通的缇扬奇要大得多，也恐怖得多的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

In a vast underground chamber, a Tiyanki stands upright, bell-shaped head swaying lightly, its wiry tentacles spread across the floor to connect with the outer walls of the chamber. It appears to be some sort of command unit, controlling and directing the growth of this planet-sized graveyard.\n\nOur [Owner.GetScientistPlural] are unable to determine what kind of creature will arise from this grim process, and when it will awaken, but one thing is for sure: it will be something far, far more vast and terrible than an ordinary Tiyanki.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:617`

## ancrel.14510.matriarch.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14510.matriarch.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个庞大的地下穴室中，一只缇扬奇竖立地站着，钟型的脑袋微微地摇晃着，它细线一样的触手舒展开来，连接着穴室的外墙。看上去它成为了某种控制单元，不断操纵、指引着这个行星级别墓地的生长。\n\n我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]没法断定这个过程什么时候会完成，但是相信一旦这只生物苏醒，它会成为远比普通的缇扬奇要大得多，也恐怖得多的存在：这只生物值得我们视作一只新的§H缇扬奇族母§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

In a vast underground chamber, a Tiyanki stands upright, bell-shaped head swaying lightly, its wiry tentacles spread across the floor to connect with the outer walls of the chamber. It appears to be some sort of command unit, controlling and directing the growth of this planet-sized graveyard.\n\nOur [Owner.GetScientistPlural] don't know when the process will be concluded, but believe once the creature awakens, it will be something far, far more vast and terrible than an ordinary Tiyanki: something worthy of being called a new §HTiyanki Matriarch§!.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:618`

## ancrel.14520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个庞大的地下穴室中，一只缇扬奇竖立地站着，钟型的脑袋微微地摇晃着，它细线一样的触手舒展开来，连接着穴室的外墙。看上去它成为了某种控制单元，不断操纵、指引着这个行星级别墓地的生长。但是这个生物现在已经死了，被我们用来前往这个地方的挖掘设备毁灭了。核心周边的外壳变得苍白而易碎，整个地方经历了第二次死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

In a vast underground chamber, a Tiyanki stands upright, bell swaying lightly, its wiry tentacles spread on the floor to connect with the outer walls of the chamber. It appeared to be some sort of control unit, controlling and directing the growth of the grave mound. But the creature is now dead, destroyed by the mining equipment we used to get access to this same place. The shells around the core are pallid and brittle; soon, this whole place will die a second death.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:627`

## ancrel.14910.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14910.name&lang=en)

### 中文

环境污染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Environmental Contamination

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:629`

## ancrel.14910.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14910.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们探险队的一名成员在探索隧道的时候突然消失了。搜寻小队花了好几天时间试图定位[Owner.GetScientist]，结果发现这人被一堵墙给包裹住，身体被触手死死地缠住。\n\n把[Owner.GetScientist]解救出来花费了不少时间和精力。[Owner.GetScientist]并不只是被简单地缠住了：这堵墙正试图在分子层面将[Owner.GetScientist]融合，使其成为墙体表面的一部分。\n\n现在[Owner.GetScientist]的状态稳定了下来，但此人拒绝离开我们的科研船——即使是在那里，也在用极度怀疑的状态死死地盯着墙壁，生怕还会有下一次袭击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

A member of our expedition team disappeared while exploring a tunnel. The retrieval squad spent days trying to locate the [Owner.GetScientist], only to find them partly encased in a wall, body tightly wrapped in tentacles.\n\nFreeing the [Owner.GetScientist] took considerable time and effort. The [Owner.GetScientist] was not simply tied: the wall was trying to fuse with the [Owner.GetScientist] at a molecular level, turning them into a part of the surface.\n\nThe [Owner.GetScientist] is now in stable condition, but refuses to leave our science ship - and even there, they keep watching the walls with extreme suspicion as though fearing another attack.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:630`

## ancrel.14910.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14910.b.evopred&lang=en)

获取生物质的一种有趣途径。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

An intriguing way to acquire biomatter.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:631`

## galactic_features.500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

繁育之所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Breeding Grounds

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:635`

## galactic_features.500.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.500.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

第一眼看过去，[From.GetName]星系就显示出强烈的敌意：所有行星都不适宜居住，不是被星系中的三个太阳轮番炙烤，就是有强烈的太阳风暴肆虐。但是，有一种生命体在这种环境下顽强地繁衍生息，那就是太空变形虫。它们放弃了原本相当孤独的生活方式，成群结队地聚在一起，在酷热的内部星系和远方的气态巨行星之间飞来飞去，这似乎是精心安排的繁殖仪式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

The [From.GetName] system gives off a strongly hostile impression on first sight: none of its planets are remotely habitable, either scorched by the searing heat of the system's three suns or stripped of life by powerful solar storms. However, there is one life form that appears to thrive in this environment - the space amoeba. Giving up their otherwise fairly solitary lifestyle, they have congregated in great numbers, flitting in and out between the blisteringly hot inner system and the large gas giant further out in what appears to be an elaborate breeding ritual.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:636`

## galactic_features.500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$galactic_features.500.desc.base$\n\n目前还不清楚是什么原因导致变形虫偏爱这种不宜居的星系。不管怎样，它们似乎意图保护自己的栖息地，并已将矛头指向[Root.GetName]，公然对其施加威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

$galactic_features.500.desc.base$\n\nIt is unclear what could possibly have caused the amoebas to favor this inhospitable system. Regardless, they seem intent on protecting it, and have already turned their flagella towards the [Root.GetName] in an overtly threatening manner.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:637`

## galactic_features.500.desc.peaceful

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.500.desc.peaceful&lang=en)

### 中文

$galactic_features.500.desc.base$\n\n目前还不清楚是什么原因导致变形虫偏爱这种不宜居的星系。幸运的是，它们似乎乐意与我们共享栖息地，没有对[Root.GetName]采取任何有敌意的措施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

$galactic_features.500.desc.base$\n\nIt is unclear what could possibly have caused the amoebas to favor this inhospitable system. Fortunately, however, they appear content to share it with us, and have made no hostile moves toward the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:638`

## galactic_features.500.alt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.500.alt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

第一眼看过去，[From.GetName]星系就显示出强烈的敌意：所有行星都不适宜居住，不是被星系中的三个太阳轮番炙烤，就是有强烈的太阳风暴肆虐。但尽管如此，这里似乎居住着大量前所未知的大型太空有机体。它们在酷热的内部星系和远方的气态巨行星之间飞来飞去，似乎在进行精心安排的繁殖仪式。\n\n目前还不清楚此星系对这些生物有什么吸引力。不管怎样，它们似乎意图保护自己的栖息地，并已将矛头指向我们，公然以一种威胁的姿态接近[Root.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

The [From.GetName] system gives off a strongly hostile impression on first sight: none of its planets are remotely habitable, either scorched by the searing heat of the system's three suns or stripped of life by powerful solar storms. However, despite all this, it seems to be inhabited in large numbers by a previously unknown large spaceborne organism, groups of which flit in and out between the blisteringly hot inner system and the large gas giant further out in what appears to be an elaborate breeding ritual.\n\nIt is unclear what attraction this system could possibly hold for these creatures. Regardless, they seem intent on protecting it, and have already turned towards us, approaching the [Root.GetName] in an overtly threatening manner.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:639`

## galactic_features.502.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.502.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[FromFrom.GetName]的新来客

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

New Arrivals in [FromFrom.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:641`

## galactic_features.502.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.502.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一大群太空变形虫已脱离其在[amoeba_home_system.GetName]星系的种群，开始独自游荡在星系之中。这些生物进入了我们在[FromFrom.GetName]星系的领地，出于天性，它们似乎无法容忍我们的存在，于是便开始攻击我们的哨站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

A large group of Space Amoebas has split off from those in the [amoeba_home_system.GetName] system and struck out to roam the galaxy on their own, entering our territory in the [FromFrom.GetName] system. As is the norm with these creatures, they seem unable to tolerate our presence and have begun attacking our outposts.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:642`

## galactic_features.502.desc.peaceful

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.502.desc.peaceful&lang=en)

### 中文

一大群太空变形虫已脱离其在[amoeba_home_system.GetName]星系的种群，开始独自游荡在星系之中。现在可以观测到，它们数量惊人，正浩浩荡荡地穿越[FromFrom.GetName]星系。幸运的是，我们已经揭开了与这些巨型生物和平共处的奥秘，因此我们不必过度担忧它们的活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

A large group of Space Amoebas has split off from those in the [amoeba_home_system.GetName] system and struck out to roam the galaxy on their own, and can now be seen crossing the [FromFrom.GetName] system in impressive numbers. Fortunately, we have uncovered the secret to peaceful coexistence with these majestic beings, so we need not be overly concerned at their movements..

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:643`

## galactic_features.502.alt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.502.alt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一大群太空有机体已脱离其在[amoeba_home_system.GetName]星系的种群，开始独自游荡在星系之中。这些生物进入了我们的领地，它们似乎无法容忍我们的存在，于是便开始攻击我们在[FromFrom.GetName]星系的哨站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

A large group of spaceborne organisms has split off from those in the [amoeba_home_system.GetName] system and struck out to roam the galaxy on their own, bringing them into our own territory. They seem unable to tolerate our presence and have begun attacking our outposts in the [FromFrom.GetName] system.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:644`

## galactic_features.402.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.402.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴袭击星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

Space Storm Hits Galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:648`

## galactic_features.402.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.402.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨型亚空间扰动已在整个星系中出现！早期报告表明星系中50%的恒星系受到这一前所未有的现象影响，原因似乎是数千年来旧有及现存文明频繁超空间航行的副作用。\n\n科学家对星系级“宇宙风暴”会在5到10年内自行消散很有信心。在那之前，风暴将对所有受影响星系内的亚光速引擎，护盾生成器及传感器系统产生严重损害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

Massive subspace disturbances have erupted in systems across the galaxy! Early reports indicate that nearly 50% of the systems in the galaxy have been affected by this unprecedented phenomenon, which appears to be a byproduct of thousands of years of heavy hyperspace travel by civilizations both past and present.\n\nScientists are confident that this galactic "space storm" will dissipate by itself within a period of 5 to 10 years. Until that happens, the storm will play havoc with sublight engines, shield generators, and sensor systems in those star systems affected by it.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:649`

## galactic_features.402.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.402.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这早晚会过去的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

This too shall pass.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:650`

## galactic_features.404.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.404.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴消散

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

Space Storm Dissipates

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:651`

## galactic_features.404.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.404.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

影响了星系中诸多区域的宇宙风暴来得快，去得也快。亚空间扰动显现的那些恒星系内的航运系统不再受到任何损害。\n\n尽管有人推测类似风暴会在数千年后最终使得超空间网络崩溃，至少现在太空航行似乎已经恢复正常了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

As quickly as it appeared, the space storm that was affecting much of the galaxy has completely dissipated. Ship systems will no longer be impaired in those systems where subspace disturbances had manifested.\n\nAlthough some speculate that storms such as this could lead to the eventual collapse of the hyperspace network thousands of years in the future, for now space travel seems to have returned to normal.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:652`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$galactic_features.402.desc$\n\n星海共同体将风暴正式命名为§H“[storm_name.GetFirstName]”§!并发布了通用旅行建议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

$galactic_features.402.desc$\n\nThe Galactic Community has officially named this storm §H"[storm_name.GetFirstName]"§! and issued a general travel advisory.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:654`

## galactic_features.406.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.406.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

影响了星系中诸多区域的§H宇宙风暴[storm_name.GetFirstName]§!来得快，去得也快。亚空间扰动显现的那些恒星系内的航运系统不再受到任何损害。\n\n尽管有人推测类似[storm_name.GetFirstName]的风暴会在数千年后最终使得超空间网络崩溃，至少现在太空航行似乎已经恢复正常了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

As quickly as it appeared, §HSpace Storm [storm_name.GetFirstName]§! has completely dissipated. Ship systems will no longer be impaired in those systems where subspace disturbances had manifested.\n\nAlthough some speculate that storms such as [storm_name.GetFirstName] could lead to the eventual collapse of the hyperspace network thousands of years in the future, for now space travel seems to have returned to normal.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:656`

## galactic_features.421.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.421.name&lang=en)

### 中文

弗雷尔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

VLUUR

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:657`

## galactic_features.421.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.421.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[From.From.From.GetName]§!星系内遭遇了某种极为强大的亚空间实体。它似乎没有敌意，对传统飞行器或许甚至没有完全察觉，但它的存在使得[From.From.From.GetName]整个恒星系中出现了强烈的亚空间扰动。\n\n这些扰动位于实体自身周围，在它离开恒星系后应该也会消失。它对我们的致意没有回复，但这个实体一直在发射模式复杂，似乎是某种语言的快子流。如果我们的翻译软件正确的话，它一遍又一遍说的是：\n\n§H“弗雷尔在寻找。弗雷尔找到了。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

An extremely powerful subspace entity of some kind has been encountered in the §H[From.From.From.GetName]§! system. It does not appear hostile and may not even be fully aware of conventional spacecraft, but its mere presence in [From.From.From.GetName] is causing massive subspace disturbances in the entire system.\n\nThese disturbances are localized around the entity itself and should disappear when it leaves the system. While it does not acknowledge hails, the entity is continuously emitting a complex pattern of tachyon pulses which appears to be a form of language. If our translation software is correct, it is saying, over and over:\n\n§H"VLUUR seeks. VLUUR finds.§!"

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:658`

## galactic_features.421.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.421.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这会干扰航运系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

This will interfere with ship systems.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:659`

## galactic_features.423.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.423.name&lang=en)

### 中文

弗雷尔的终结

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

The End of VLUUR

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:660`

## galactic_features.423.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.423.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在与§H[vluur_system.GetName]§!星系中我方军队交战时，亚空间实体§H“弗雷尔”§!提高了其发送快子流的频率，但不停重复的信息与之前一样：\n\n§H“弗雷尔在寻找。弗雷尔找到了。”§!\n\n在受到致命一击的同时，这个实体自爆了，大爆炸使得整个恒星系中撒满了暗物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

Throughout its battle with our forces in the §H[vluur_system.GetName]§! system, the subspace entity §H"VLUUR"§! increased the frequency at which it was emitting tachyon pulses, but the endlessly repeating message remained the same:\n\n§H"VLUUR seeks. VLUUR finds."§!\n\nWhen the final killing blow was delivered, the entity detonated in a massive explosion that showered the system in dark matter.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:661`

### English

We shall collect what we can.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:662`

## pirate.155.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.155.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]代表：$pirate.155.proclamation$\n\n我们理解，关卡检查有时会让人精神崩的紧紧的。只要我们相互合作，就不会有人受到伤害。请放心，我们会将正式占有的每一粒谷物和每一块矿石充分利用在[thalassocracy_species.GetName]生态居住繁殖计划中。我方对你方的贴心表示无比的感激。\n\n$pirate.155.thalassocracy_signoff$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

Representatives of the [root.GetName]: $pirate.155.proclamation$\n\nSometimes these customs checks can be stressful. We understand. Co-operate, and no one need be harmed. And please, rest assured that every grain and ore we solemnly claim will be put to good use within the [thalassocracy_species.GetName] eco-hab propagation scheme. We are grateful for your consideration.\n\n$pirate.155.thalassocracy_signoff$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:670`

## pirate.155.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.155.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]的构造体代表：封锁现已在[thalassocracy_target_system.GetName]生效。从此以后，该区域的所有机械单位都将成为[thalassocracy_country.GetName]的臣民，为我们服务。反抗的舰队将遭到武力镇压。\n\n我们理解此举可能会对你国造成物质上的极大不便。但我们保证，只要我们的$name_thalassocracy_agency$军官达到其指定配额，我们就会停止此举措。你一定对我们这样的指令系统面临的压力能够表示理解。\n\n在[thalassocracy_species.GetName]的生态居住繁殖图景中，你方重新编程的无人机将承担崇高的使命。$pirate.155.thalassocracy_signoff$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

Representative construct of the [root.GetName]: a blockade is now in effect at [thalassocracy_target_system.GetName]. Any and all machine units in this region are henceforth subjects of the [thalassocracy_country.GetName]; they will be made to service us. Resisting fleets will be fired upon.\n\nWe appreciate that this operation may represent a material inconvenience to you. We assure you that it will conclude, once our $name_thalassocracy_agency$ officers meet their designated quotas. Surely you can understand the pressures of a directive like ours.\n\nA worthy sense of purpose awaits your re-programmed drones within the [thalassocracy_species.GetName] eco-hab propagation scheme. $pirate.155.thalassocracy_signoff$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:671`

## pirate.155.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.155.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]实体代表：$pirate.155.proclamation$\n\n我们理解，关卡检查有时会让人精神崩的紧紧的。只要我们相互合作，就能防止你们的[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]受伤，否则，它们可能会遇到麻烦。请放心，我们会将占有的每一粒谷物和每一块矿石充分利用在[thalassocracy_species.GetName]生态居住繁殖计划中。希望你们能共同考虑此提议，我们感激不尽。\n\n$pirate.155.thalassocracy_signoff$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

Representative entity of the [root.GetName]: $pirate.155.proclamation$\n\nSometimes these customs checks can be stressful. We understand. Co-operate, and no [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] need be harmed or otherwise inconvenienced. And please, rest assured that every grain and ore we claim will go to good homes within the [thalassocracy_species.GetName] eco-hab propagation scheme. We are grateful for your collective consideration.\n\n$pirate.155.thalassocracy_signoff$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:672`

## pirate.155.proclamation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.155.proclamation&lang=en)

### 中文

暂时封锁现已在[thalassocracy_target_system.GetName]生效。在此星系加工和运输的食物及矿物现在会被[thalassocracy_country.GetName]$name_thalassocracy_agency$军官查封。所有非商用飞船将遭到驱逐，若执意前行则会受到炮火攻击。这些条件将一直生效，直至我们完成$name_thalassocracy_agency_abbreviated$配额。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

a temporary blockade is now in effect at [thalassocracy_target_system.GetName]. Processing and transportation of food and minerals through this system are now subject to seizure by $name_thalassocracy_agency$ officers of the [thalassocracy_country.GetName]. All non-merchant vessels will be turned away, or else fired upon. These conditions shall remain in effect until our $name_thalassocracy_agency_abbreviated$ quotas can be met.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:673`

## federations3.2001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我国各处的多个研究站探测到了以[root.Capital.GetName]的恒星基地为中心，超空间中存在的轻微变形。这一效应在“真空”中表现为局部的类极光现象，居住在恒星基地上的人员将其描述为一种色彩鲜艳的气态卷须，包裹着整个空间站。\n\n此外，我们在[root.GetAllianceName]中的盟友报告称他们的首都星系出现了类似现象。总统[discloud_coordinator.ruler.GetRegnalName]已呼吁立即对此事开展合作调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

Various research stations across our empire have detected subtle distortions in hyperspace, centered upon the starbase at [root.Capital.GetName]. The effect presents in 'real space' as a localized aurora, which starbase inhabitants describe as brightly-colored, gaseous tendrils enveloping the station.\n\nFurthermore: our allies in the [root.GetAllianceName] report similar phenomena in their capital systems. President [discloud_coordinator.ruler.GetRegnalName] has called for an immediate and coordinated investigation into this matter.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:698`

## dismembered_cloud_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adismembered_cloud_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

云团簇聚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

Flocks of Cloud

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:700`

## dismembered_cloud_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adismembered_cloud_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

色彩鲜艳的卷须状云团聚集在我国首都星系周围。我们的盟友报告称在他们首都也出现了类似效应。是什么导致了这些现象？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

Brightly-colored tendrils of cloud have gathered around the starbase in our capital system. Our allies report similar effects at their capitals. What's causing these phenomena?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:701`

## DISMEMBERED_CLOUD_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADISMEMBERED_CLOUD_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

云遮雾绕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

Cloud, Snagged

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:703`

## DISMEMBERED_CLOUD_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADISMEMBERED_CLOUD_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

色彩鲜艳的卷须状云团封闭了我们在[root.Capital.GetName]的恒星基地，以一种缺少规律——或许甚至是有生命——的方式从亚空间中来去。是什么导致了这种情况？\n\n这一项目是[root.GetAllianceName]成员国进行的更广泛的科学研究的一部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

Brightly-colored tendrils of cloud have latched onto our starbase at [root.Capital.GetName], phasing in and out of hyperspace in an erratic - or perhaps even organic - manner. What's causing this?\n\nThis project is part of a wider scientific effort being conducted by members of the [root.GetAllianceName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:704`

## tooltip_dismemberedcloud_begin

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atooltip_dismemberedcloud_begin&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetAllianceName]各成员国获得一个§H特殊项目§!，以对这些现象间存在的关联进行联合调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

Each member of the [root.GetAllianceName] will be tasked with a §HSpecial Project§! - a joint effort to find what links these phenomena.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:705`

## federations3.2010.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[root.Capital.GetName]中极光状云团的调查解决了一些问题，但提出了更多疑问。这些形态各异的卷须状云团会在我们向盟友的研究队伍发出通讯光束后出现，但也只有这样才会出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

Our investigation of the aurora-like cloud at [root.Capital.GetName] raised more questions than answers. Distinct tendrils of cloud form whenever we beam communications to our allies' research teams - but only then.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:707`

## federations3.2010.red.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.red.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$每朵云都略带红色，边缘凹凸不平，移动方式躁动。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ Each is tinged with red, appearing jagged at the edges - and they move in an agitated manner.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:708`

## federations3.2010.yellow.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.yellow.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$每朵云都是黄色，边缘模糊不清。它们缓缓移动，如同被微风吹拂一般。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ Each one appears yellow, with hazily-defined edges. The whisps move lethargically, as though caught in a gentle breeze.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:709`

## federations3.2010.green.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.green.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$这些云看起来是生意盎然的绿色，这些蒸汽的移动似乎有目的性，以某种方式示意着什么。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ Appearing to us in vibrant green, these vapors' movements have been described as somehow beckoning and purposeful.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:710`

## federations3.2010.blue.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.blue.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$这些蓝色隆起似乎有些害羞难觅，在被直接观察到时总是躲回超空间之中。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ These blue protruberances seem somehow elusive or shy, phasing back into hyperspace whenever they are observed directly.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:711`

## federations3.2010.indigo.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.indigo.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$云朵上清晰可辨的紫色“附肢”缓缓移动，专门瞄着恒星基地外表的各种部件，仿佛在对其进行评估。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ Each vivid, purple 'appendage' of the cloud moves slowly and purposefully to point at various features on the exterior of the starbase, as though appraising it.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:712`

## federations3.2010.pink.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.pink.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$这些朦胧的粉色蒸汽团据说很“顽皮”，会从偶然接近的舰船旁边快速逃开。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ These pink and hazy vapors have been described as 'playful', skittering away from any ships which happen to approach.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:713`

## federations3.2010.gray.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.gray.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$它们存在的时间较长，其后留下似乎是灰色的烟迹。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ They remain present for extended periods, leaving behind trails of what appears to be gray smoke.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:714`

## federations3.2010.orange.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.orange.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$这些体积很大的橙色弧状云团喜欢在我们恒星基地的下侧弯曲，使得恒星基地如同着火了一般。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ These sweeping, orange curves will tend to curl around the underside of our starbase, giving it the appearance of being on fire.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:715`

## federations3.2010.brown.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.brown.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$它们深棕的色调通常并不透光；遮蔽了恒星基地上正常情况下能看到的灿烂星光。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ Their deep brown hues seem unusually opaque; they obscure much of the starscape one would normally see from within the starbase.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:716`

## federations3.2010.cyan.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.cyan.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2010.intro$气体团内明晰的青色色调使其外表如同行星上的极光一般——不过它的色带会自发从曲线变幻成直角，如同要组成数码波一般。\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

$federations3.2010.intro$ A vivid, cyan tint within the gas gives it the appearance of a planetary aurora - only its various bands will spontaneously switch from sweeping curves to right-angles, as though forming a digital wave.\n\n$federations3.2010.close$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:717`

## federations3.2010.close

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2010.close&lang=en)

### 中文

单靠我们自己无法从这些行为中分辨出进一步的含义——但在§Y全体成员国结果汇总后§!，[root.GetAllianceName]应该会有更多答案。\n\n初期理论猜测这些跨维度实体以某种方式与我们的超空间通讯频段产生了谐振。这次意外的引诱或许可以用来进一步利用这些实体进行研究，或者我们可以切换频率，希望这些云团会消散。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

On our own, we are unable to discern any further meaning from these behaviors - but the [root.GetAllianceName] expects to have more answers once §Yevery member's results are collated§!.\n\nEarly theories suggest these dimension-spanning entities somehow harmonize with our hyperspace communication bands. This accidental lure could be kept in place in order to further exploit the entity for study, or we could switch frequencies in the hopes of dispersing it.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:718`

放它自由。

把它留在这儿。

## federations3.2015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被解体的云团

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

The Dismembered Cloud

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:721`

## federations3.2015.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2015.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在对我们的研究结果进行比对后发现，这些在我们联邦成员国首都盘绕的、看似没有知觉的极光状云团事实上组成了一个单一的有生命实体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:638`

### English

The results of our findings have been collated: it would seem that every instance of barely-sentient aurora cloud at our federation's capitals was, in fact, part of a single, living entity.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:722`

## federations3.2015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2015.intro$\n\n研究者们能得出的结果如下：这个居住在超空间的实体因为事故漂移到了我们联邦的宇域，它被“钩在”我们最为繁忙（也就是为我们首都的恒星基地提供服务）的太空通讯中继器上。研究者一致同意这个气态实体之前正§H尝试重组自身§!，如果不是现在的状况，它处于碎片状态下是做不到这件事的。\n\n在重组完成后，这朵云消失了，它回到了超空间中——但在此之前它到访了我们联盟中的许多研究站。在每个研究站，它多次准确地穿入穿出超空间，在旁观者看来如同舞蹈一般。云朵展示了它穿越超空间屏障的本能，这为我们提供了无价的数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

$federations3.2015.intro$\n\nAs best the researchers can tell: this hyperspace-dwelling entity drifted into our federation space by accident, whereupon it became 'snagged' on our busiest, spaceborne communications relays - those which serve our capital starbases. Researchers agree that the gaseous entity was §Hattempting to reconstitute itself§! - a task it could otherwise not achieve in such a fragmented state.\n\nAfter it reformed, the cloud disappeared back into hyperspace - but not before visiting a number of our alliance's research stations. At each one, it performed a number of precise movements in and out of hyperspace - a display which some witnesses describe as resembling a dance. These demonstrations of the cloud's innate ability to traverse the hyperspace barrier have provided us all with invaluable data.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:723`

## federations3.2016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2015.intro$\n\n我们收集到的数据并不完整，但足以推断出我们遭遇了一个格式塔存在：这个气态存在适于居住在超空间中。项目合作者提出理论，认为这朵云的各个部分在漂移过我们星系时被“钩在”了首都的恒星基地上。它似乎想要§H重组自身§!——但由于我方没有一致为其提供帮助，这朵云的某些部分渐渐散去，不再能参与到重组中了。\n\n那些成功重组的部分随后消失去了超空间之中；余下的部分可以继续为研究者提供关于超空间的、有趣的遥测数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

$federations3.2015.intro$\n\nThe data we have is incomplete, but is enough to suggest we have encountered a gestalt being: gaseous in form, and adapted to dwelling in hyperspace. Project coordinators theorize that various parts of the cloud became 'snagged' on our capital starbases whilst it drifted through our galaxy. It appears to have wanted to §Hreconstitute itself§! - but without a united effort on our part, some of those clouds have begun to lose cohesion, and may no longer be able to rejoin.\n\nThose parts of the cloud which did reunite soon vanished into hyperspace; what remains continues to provide researchers with interesting telemetry on matters relating to hyperspace.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:724`

## federations3.2016.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2016.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这样的话，或许能从中得到某些益处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

Some benefit may yet be gleaned, then.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:725`

## federations3.2017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于我们没有对这一项目进行合作研究，关于这个造访我们首都的跨维度云团，我们很难确切得到些什么。理论众说纷纭，但我们都观察到这些云团变得相当躁动。\n\n它们的物理结构开始解体，我们注意到这些云团对于刺激的反应也减少了。它们开始更为迅速地穿入穿出超空间。这在我们的通讯频段上产生了显著的静态干扰，但也为[root.Capital.GetName]的理论研究者提供了宝贵的数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

With no collaborative effort having been applied this project, little can be said with any certainty about the pan-dimensional cloud visiting our capitals. Theories abound, but all of us are now witness to these clouds' stormy agitation.\n\nAs their physical structure breaks down, we note that the clouds become less responsive to stimuli. They have begun phasing in and out of hyperspace much more rapidly. This is generating a significant amount of static on many of our communications bands, but it is also providing valuable data for our theoretical researchers in [root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:726`

## federations3.2017.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2017.a.tooltip&lang=en)

§H[Root.GetAllianceName]§!收到临时的团结度增益。\n\n

### English

§H[Root.GetAllianceName]§! receives a temporary boost to cohesion.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:727`

Cloud's Boon

## joint_cloud_boon_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajoint_cloud_boon_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过协助一团造访我们联邦成员国首都、居住于超空间的气态实体，我们已经获得了对于超光速生物学及理论物理学的独特洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

By aiding a gaseous, hyperspace-dwelling entity which once visited our federation's capitals, we have been granted unique insights into superluminary biology and theoretical physics.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:730`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atemp_cloud_boon&lang=en)

被解体的云团

### English

Dismembered Cloud
