## temp_cloud_boon_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atemp_cloud_boon_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们联邦成员国首都与一团居住于超空间的气态来访者的偶然邂逅，这给予了我们关于超光速物理学的多种洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

A chance encounter with a gaseous, hyperspace-dwelling visitor to our federation's capitals has granted us various insights into superluminary physics.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:732`

## pm_joint_cloud_snag

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apm_joint_cloud_snag&lang=en)

### 中文

被捕捉的云团

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

A Cloud Ensnared

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:733`

## pm_joint_cloud_snag_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apm_joint_cloud_snag_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们牺牲了这一区域的某些信号保真性，以换取对一个被我们恒星基地信号传输引诱来的、居于超空间的生物及超光速物理学的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

We have sacrificed some signal fidelity in this region in return for insights into superluminary physics, courtesy of a hyperspace-dwelling creature which was lured in by our starbase transmissions.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:734`

## federations3.2103.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2103.name&lang=en)

### 中文

共振信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

A Resonating Signal

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:740`

## federations3.2103.desc.body

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2103.desc.body&lang=en)

### 中文

驶入[event_target:leylines_world.GetName]大气层的短程飞船报告，称其舰体上有奇怪的声音。声音的来源似乎在行星的地表之下，其所在地区富含稀有矿物。\n\n我们在[from.Federation.GetName]的同事监督的星球也报告过类似事件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

Short-range vessels whose routes take them into the atmosphere of [event_target:leylines_world.GetName] report a strange sound assaulting their hulls. The source seems to lie just below the planet's surface, in an area rich in unusual minerals.\n\nSimilar occurrences have also been reported on worlds overseen by our colleagues in the [from.Federation.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:741`

## federations3.2103.desc.generic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2103.desc.generic&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2103.desc.body$为应对此事，他们组成了一个由[event_target:leylines_coordinator.GetName]领导的委员会，负责协调我们都想要参与的挖掘工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

$federations3.2103.desc.body$ They have responded by forming a committee led by [event_target:leylines_coordinator.GetName], there to co-ordinate any excavation effort we all may wish to take part in.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:742`

## federations3.2103.desc.coordinator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2103.desc.coordinator&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations3.2103.desc.body$为应对此事，他们让我们领导委员会，协调我们都想要参与的挖掘工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

$federations3.2103.desc.body$ They have responded by tasking us with leadership of a committee, coordinating any excavation effort we all may wish to take part in.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:743`

## leylines_digsite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_digsite&lang=en)

### 中文

地狱嗡鸣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

Chthonic Siren

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:744`

## leylines_digsite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_digsite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾经以为，这不过是位于[From.Planet.GetName]上的鸡肋矿物层。但现在，这片矿物层却开始发出震耳欲聋的奇怪嗡嗡声。这还不是个例，联邦境内的诸星球上均可以听到类似的声音。这一异常现象背后究竟隐藏着什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

What was once thought to be merely a seam of half-interesting minerals on [From.Planet.GetName] has begun emitting a bizarre and deafening hum - and it's not alone. Worlds across federation space are being buffeted by a similar noise. Whatever could be behind these singular phenomena?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:745`

## federations3.2104.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2104.name&lang=en)

### 中文

附近的共振信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

A Resonating Signal Nearby

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:747`

## federations3.2104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[root.Federation.GetName]的同事报告称听到了震耳欲聋的嗡嗡声，他认为声音的来源应该在地表之下，至少存在于一个星球中。他们成立了一个研究委员会，旨在支持他们的挖掘工作。\n\n他们要求我们提供自己的额外资源，以便我们能够更好地理解这件奇怪的麻烦事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

Our colleagues in the [root.Federation.GetName] report a deafening hum from sources thought to lie buried on at least one of their worlds. A research committee has been established to support any excavation efforts they make.\n\nWe have been asked to provide our own, additional resources, in order that we might all better understand this strange and inconvenient occurrence.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:748`

## federations3.2104.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2104.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会尽自己所能提供支持。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

We will lend what support we can.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:749`

## leylines_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

地下的噪音

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

The Din from Below

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:750`

## leylines_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的联邦境内，至少有一颗行星发出震耳欲聋的嗡嗡声。由于我们的同事正在努力挖掘这一遗迹，他们要求我们提供自己的科学资源支持其工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

A deafening hum rings out across at least one planet in our federation's space. As our colleagues work to excavate these sites, we have been asked to lend our own scientific resources to the effort.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:751`

## LEYLINES_ACOUSTIC_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEYLINES_ACOUSTIC_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

应对共振和声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

Combatting Resonant Harmonics

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:752`

## LEYLINES_ACOUSTIC_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEYLINES_ACOUSTIC_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的联邦同事正在极度嘈杂的环境中努力进行挖掘工作，为协助他们，我们的任务是改进所有人的隔音罩。考古学家目前只能断断续续地轮班，暂时还没有出现身体不适的迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

In order to assist our federation colleagues amidst exceedingly noisy conditions, we have been tasked with refining their personnel's acoustic shields. The archaeologists are currently unable to work anything more than staccato shifts before they show signs of physical distress.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:753`

## federations3.2124.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2124.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们未能向同事提供他们要求的额外隔音罩。好在设备匮乏只会阻碍他们的工作，并不会彻底阻止他们挖掘。但是，这还是让一线研究人员更加不安。纵使报告显示噪音现在正自己减弱，他们的情绪也没有任何好转。\n\n[root.Federation.GetName]项目委员会现在邀请我们研究声音本身，看看能否得到些许启发，以此来弥补我们之前的过失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

We have failed to provide our colleagues with the additional acoustic shielding they had requested. This lack of equipment will only hamper their efforts, and won't stall them outright. Nevertheless, it has left their front-line researchers that bit more rattled, even after reports which suggest that the noise is now subsiding of its own accord.\n\nThe [root.Federation.GetName] project committee now invites us to make amends by contributing to a study of the sound itself, to see if that may shed any light.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:754`

## federations3.2124.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2124.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要竭尽全力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

We shall endeavor.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:755`

## federations3.2125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

改良型隔音罩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

Improved Noise Cancellation

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:756`

## federations3.2125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已成功提高了折叠隔音罩的覆盖范围和适应力，并与[root.Federation.GetName]的同事共享了这些升级成果。他们的一线研究员报告称意识不清和身体剧痛的情况已经有了极大改善。协调委员会现在要求我们协助进行数据分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:669`

### English

We have succeeded in improving the reach and resilience of our deployable acoustic shields. These upgrades have been shared with our colleagues in the [root.Federation.GetName]; their researchers on the ground already report a steep decline in cognitive distress and deep-body aching. The co-ordination committee now asks us to assist with a matter of data analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:757`

## LEYLINES_ANALYSIS_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEYLINES_ANALYSIS_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

共振嗡鸣分析

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

Resonant Hum Analysis

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:758`

## LEYLINES_ANALYSIS_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEYLINES_ANALYSIS_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[root.Federation.GetName]的同事已经开始进行下一阶段的挖掘工作，但是每个受影响的星球仍然会发出噪音，因此项目监督委员会委托我们研究这些逐渐减弱的噪音。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

Now that our colleagues in the [root.Federation.GetName] have begun the next stage of their excavations, the project's oversight committee has delegated us to study the waning din which still emanates from each affected world.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:759`

## federations3.2129.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2129.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们未能得出令人满意的成果，[root.Federation.GetName]项目委员会对此表示失望。困扰联邦内多个星球的噪音终于消失，但研究机会的窗口也随之关闭。研究人员尽自己所能收集了一些信息，但现在只能寄希望于现场的考古学家能够给出详细的答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

The [root.Federation.GetName] project committee expresses its disappointment in our lack of results. As the din which has afflicted a number of our federated worlds finally dimmed, so too did the window of research opportunities close. Researchers gathered what insights they could, but any hope for detailed answers now rests solely with the archaeologists on the ground.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:760`

## federations3.2130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

共振嗡鸣已经过分析

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

Resonant Hum, Analyzed

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:761`

## federations3.2130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

联邦境内处考古遗址中的嗡鸣已经开始减弱。通过研究每种声音的录音，并将我们与同事的数据进行交叉对比，我们发现，当震耳欲聋的嗡鸣相互重叠时，会产生惊艳绝伦的和声，旋律可绕梁三日，令人心旷神怡。对于建造这些遗址的人来说，这种设计可能具有文化意义，但我们可能永远无法了解其中的含义。研究仍在继续……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

The humming at archaeological sites across our federation's space has begun to subside. By studying recordings of each sound - and cross-referencing our data with that of our colleagues - we discovered a surprising harmony as each deafening blast overlaps to form a more pleasant, ambient melody. It's possible that this arrangement held cultural significance for whoever developed these sites; we may never know. The research continues...

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:762`

## federations3.2105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

声震屋瓦的贝壳形建筑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

Sonorous Shells

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:764`

## federations3.2105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们必须断断续续地轮班工作，但从技术上讲，揭开这个遗址的奥秘非常简单。我们挖开了两层平平无奇的岩石，然后才看到一个大贝壳，也可以说是穹顶。此外，附近有一些相同的穹顶也已部分出土，目前看来，这些穹顶似乎排列成弧形或圆形。\n\n每个穹顶都是用模子建成，并且进行过一定程度的加固，能够经受地质年代的考验。每个建筑都有会议室大小，因此应该能够进入其中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

The work of uncovering this site was technically straightforward - despite our having to work in staccato shifts. We removed two featureless strata before encountering a large shell or dome. Other, identical domes have been partially uncovered nearby; so far they seem to be arranged in an arc or circle.\n\nEach dome was constructed from a mold, and was cured to such a degree that they have withstood geological ages. Ingress to these structures - each of which is the size of a gathering hall - should be possible.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:765`

## federations3.2111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2111.name&lang=en)

### 中文

安静的避难所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

Quiet Refuge

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:766`

## federations3.2111.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2111.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们尚未确定无处不在的嗡鸣的来源，但其主导地位已经开始有所减弱。自此以后，我们的研究人员就能够专注突破这座类似半球的建筑。\n\n里面的空间完全没有实际内容，我们初步结论为，此建筑是按感官隔离室设计的。此外，穹顶的内表面似乎画有星座图，辅以神话人物和某种形式的经文。我们正在努力解读这些设计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

While we have yet to identify the source of the pervasive humming, its dominating presence has begun to wane. Our researchers were since able to focus on breaching the near-hemispherical structure.\n\nThe space within is entirely devoid of material contents; our initial conclusion is that this was designed as a sensory isolation chamber. Furthermore, the inside surface of the dome is illustrated with what appear to be constellation charts, supplemented by mythical figures and some form of scripture. We are already working to interpret these designs.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:767`

## federations3.2112.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2112.name&lang=en)

### 中文

贝壳形建筑的内部结构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

The Shell's Innards

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:768`

## federations3.2112.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2112.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在穹顶内部发现了大量精巧的设备，但没有一台能够产生我们在外面还能听到的巨大噪音。一支小规模的工作小队已经独立出去，继续我们的调查工作。\n\n大部分研究人员还留在这个贝壳形结构中，研究其内部机制，设备结构复杂，装饰美观，乍一看像是某种大型计算器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

We discovered a plethora of elaborate equipment inside the dome, none of which seems capable of producing the immense noise we can still hear outside. A small task force has split off to continue that aspect of our investigation.\n\nThe bulk of our researchers remain within the shell-like structure, studying the mechanisms within. The equipment is intricate, decorative in form, and seems at first glance to be some form of vast calculator.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:769`

## federations3.2116.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2116.name&lang=en)

### 中文

占星之屋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

The Astrolochamber

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:770`

## federations3.2116.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2116.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[From.Owner.Federation.GetName]各地的报告都确认：嗡鸣现已消失。尽管各星际遗址之间距离遥远，但嗡鸣声几乎在同一时间停止。\n\n从那以后，我们的研究团队依据穹顶内部描绘的众多星座推断出了一些模型，这些星座通常用于占星预测。研究人员收集的数据表明，此设施，若是按年代排序，比周围地层要古老得多——但有一处明显的例外：研究人员偶然在穹顶外面发现了一个大型祭坛，这表明在相对较近的年代，至少有一个文明曾使用过这一空间。\n\n祭台使用当地的石头凿成，上面雕有蚀刻图，我们认为这些蚀刻图是用来翻译穹顶上的古老星图，将其转化一个现今我们更为熟悉的星图。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Reports from across the [From.Owner.Federation.GetName] confirm: the droning sirens have now dissipated. They ceased almost simultaneously, despite the vast distances between interstellar sites.\n\nSince that time, our research team has extrapolated models from the many constellations depicted on the dome's interior, which seems to have been used to make astrological predictions. The data they've gathered suggests that this facility is far older than the surrounding strata, by an order of epochs - with one notable exception: the chance discovery of a large dais immediately outside the dome suggests that at least one civilization has used this space comparatively recently.\n\nThe dais was hewn from local stone, and is decorated with etchings we believe were used to translate the dome's outdated star charts for a galaxy arranged much as it is for us, now.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:771`

## federations3.2117.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2117.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星系之内还有星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Star Systems Within Star Systems

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:772`

## federations3.2117.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2117.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[From.Owner.Federation.GetName]各地的报告都表明：困扰我们共有空间各处研究遗址的嗡鸣声已经消失。这让我们的研究人员如释重负，因为他们可以开始研究穹顶内安置的复杂设备了。\n\n尽管其设计古怪之极，但我们仍然辨认出，该设备由相互连接的太阳系仪组成。这些模型描绘了整个[From.Planet.System.GetName]星系和另外两个恒星系，但我们以及联邦内的同事至今仍不知道这两个星系的名称和位置。每台太阳系仪都由一组轴和齿轮驱动，支撑着无数由方解石和石化树液制成的雕塑。尽管看起来一动不动，但实际上它们正以缓慢的速度移动——就像巨大的星系时钟的一枝一节，而时钟的“钟声”可能就是我们先前听到的巨大嗡鸣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Reports from across the [From.Owner.Federation.GetName] confirm: the ringing hum which was plaguing research sites across our shared space has now dissipated. This came as relief to our researchers as they investigated the complex equipment arranged inside the dome.\n\nAlthough its design is eccentric in the extreme, we've identified it as an arrangement of interlinked orreries. These models depict the entirety of [From.Planet.System.GetName] along with two other star systems, whose names and positions are not yet known - to us nor our federation colleagues. Each is driven by a nest of axles and gears, supporting myriad sculptures in calcite and fossilized sap. Although they appear quite still, they are actually moving at a glacial pace - like parts of a vast galactic timepiece, whose 'chimes' might be the great siren we heard earlier.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:773`

## federations3.2120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天降祝福

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

Genius Caeli

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:774`

## federations3.2120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[from.Planet.GetName]和其他地方穹顶的研究已结束。每个遗址都是巨大的占星设备，跨越星际的距离与其他设备同步。\n\n我们的研究表明，这类设施被视为宇宙能量的汇聚点，影响着建造设施的古老文明的总体“位置感”。蚀刻记录中提到，议会将“引导”这些能量，从而指引其帝国的命运。\n\n他们的社会最终依然走向衰落，这种仪式并没有为其逆天改命。但在之后的一千年中，出现了一个继业的文明，这一继承者文明翻译并恢复了这一仪式。那之后，他们也离开了这些地方，不知去了何处。\n\n这块巨大的时钟至少已经见证了两个文明的兴衰。现在，时钟的自动嗡鸣召唤着我们，让我们在太空区域各处见证这一仪式。无论是否会有确切结果，至少[from.owner.Federation.GetName]已经准备好进行观察了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

Our study of the domes on [From.Planet.GetName] and elsewhere has reached an end. Each site is a vast astrological device, synchronized with others like it across interstellar distances.\n\nOur studies suggest that facilities like this one were treated as focal points for cosmic energy, affecting a general 'sense of place' for the ancient civilization which built them. Etched records speak of councils who would 'channel' these energies in order to guide their empire's fortunes.\n\nSuch observances did not halt their society's eventual decline, but in the millennia which followed there also came an inheritor who would translate and revive the practice. They too have since left these sites, for parts unknown.\n\nThe vast timepiece has slowly ticked on through the periods of at least two civilizations; its automated siren has now called on us to observe this ritual at sites across our region of space. Whether or not anything tangible results from it, the [from.owner.Federation.GetName] is now poised to observe...

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:775`

## federations3.2135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

运转中的遗迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

A Legacy in Motion

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:777`

## federations3.2135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有人可能会说，我们直到最后一刻才收集完数据。联邦最新成果刚刚发布没几天，[from.Federation.GetName]境内几个圆顶遗址就突然活跃起来，一束束光从地下深处飞流直上，正好映照在我们首都上方，这让人难以置信。\n\n我们的研究人员也很困惑，遗址的建造者怎么会知道这些？但是[root.Capital.GetName]的人民更关心一连串梦幻般的景象，所有人都亲眼见证了这短暂的景象。广播只传达了一个目的，即宣告这件事成为“§H求索的时刻§!”。\n\n没有关于后续现象的报告，圆顶遗址又一次变暗，继续缓慢地像时钟一样滴答地走过一个个时代，当然也包括我们当下的时代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

One might say that the last of our data was gathered in the nick of time. Mere days after the last of our federation's findings was published, each domed site within [from.Federation.GetName] space burst into life. Cascades of light spilled upwards from sources deep underground, their beams cast squarely - and improbably - at our capitals.\n\nOur researchers are puzzled - how could the makers of these sites know? - but those on [root.Capital.GetName] are more concerned with a swath of dreamlike visions which briefly appeared on every device with a visual output. The broadcast 'spoke' with singular purpose, proclaiming this to be a '§HTime of Knowing§!'.\n\nNo subsequent phenomena have been reported; the domed sites dimmed again, and have resumed their glacial 'ticking' through ages which now include our own.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:778`

## leylines_mod_knowing

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_mod_knowing&lang=en)

### 中文

天降祝福：求索的时刻

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

Genius Caeli: A Time of Knowing

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:779`

## leylines_mod_knowing_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_mod_knowing_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们合作研究了一台奇怪的占星设备，从而获得了新的认知：古老的唯心主义社会有关扩展知识的预测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

Our collaborative study of a strange, astrological device has granted us insight: the knowledge-expanding predictions of an ancient, spiritualist society.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:780`

## federations3.2136.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2136.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一刹那，[from.Federation.GetName]境内几座圆顶遗址突然活跃起来。据报告，一束束光从地下深处的源头倾泻而出，正好映照在我们首都上方，这简直不可思议。\n\n我们的研究人员也很困惑，遗址的建造者怎么会知道这些？但是[root.Capital.GetName]的人民更关心一连串梦幻般的景象，所有人都亲眼见证了这短暂的景象。尽管还不清楚广播的目的，但人们共同见证这一景象凸显出一种§H平衡繁荣§!的情感氛围。\n\n没有关于后续现象的报告，圆顶遗址又一次变暗，继续神秘而缓慢地像时钟一样滴答作响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

Each of the once-noisy, domed sites within [from.Federation.GetName] space has, for a brief moment, burst into life. Cascades of light were reported spilling out from sources deep underground, their beams cast squarely - and improbably - at our capitals.\n\nOur researchers are puzzled - how could the makers of these sites know? - but those on [root.Capital.GetName] are more concerned with a swath of dreamlike visions which briefly appeared on every device with a visual output. It's unclear what the broadcast was intended for, but a commonly-shared observation highlights feelings of §Hbalanced prosperity§!.\n\nNo subsequent phenomena have been reported; the domed sites dimmed again, and have resumed their mysterious, glacial 'ticking'.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:781`

## leylines_mod_lagom

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_mod_lagom&lang=en)

### 中文

天降祝福：平衡的繁荣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

Genius Caeli: Balanced Prosperity

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:782`

## leylines_mod_lagom_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleylines_mod_lagom_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们合作研究了一台奇怪的占星设备，从而获得了新的认知：古老的唯心主义社会有关可持续性的教训。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

Our collaborative study of a strange, astrological device has granted us insight: lessons in sustainability, from an ancient, spiritualist society.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:783`

## federations3.2137.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2137.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不可知的遗迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

An Unknowable Legacy

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:784`

## federations3.2137.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2137.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

整个[from.Federation.GetName]的智能生物都为眼前突然出现的宏伟景象所震惊。与此同时，几年前发现的古老圆顶遗址突然活跃起来。据报告，一束束光从地下深处的源头倾泻而出，正好映照在我们首都上方，这简直不可思议。\n\n我们在[root.Capital.GetName]的研究人员对这种梦幻般的景象感到非常困惑。目前我们还不清楚这一现象的目的，也没有观测到后续现象，因此无法获得更多线索。我们似乎错过了对其加以研究的时机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

Sapients throughout the [from.Federation.GetName] have been startled by a sudden, apparent hijacking of visual feeds which coincided with a burst of activity from ancient, domed sites discovered some years ago. Cascades of light were reported spilling out from sources deep underground, their beams cast squarely - and improbably - at our capitals.\n\nOur researchers on [root.Capital.GetName] are puzzled by this broadcast of dreamlike visions. It's unclear what it was intended for, and no subsequent phenomena have been reported which might lend us more clues. It would seem that we have simply missed an opportunity to study this phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:785`

## fedev.605.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.605.name&lang=en)

### 中文

即将进行的试点：物流重构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

Upcoming Trial: Logistics Remodelling

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:791`

## fedev.605.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.605.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

各种货物的运输路线是我们贸易联邦兴旺繁荣的生命线。但迄今为止，各帝国仍在容器设计、装载程序及运输管道等方面采用各自的物流标准。\n\n为了使效率最大化，一个新的试点项目将在几年后实施——项目目标是调研并试验一种全新、具体，适用于联邦所有成员国的运输标准。\n\n参与试点需要拥有一个至少有§H50点贸易额§!流经的恒星系。此外，设有§H外星贸易公司§!意味着更高的统计数据，所以应对其进行鼓励。\n\n由于项目所涉范围广泛，一些开销必不可少。但这些牺牲是为了长远的利益。换句话说，也就是更多的未来利润。\n\n联邦各成员国均被强烈鼓励参与项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

The routing of various goods is the life-blood of our prosperous trade federation. But so far each empire has relied on their own logistic standards with regards to container design, loading procedures, and shipping pipelines.\n\nIn order to maximize efficiency, a new trial project will commence in a few years - one that aims to investigate and trial a new, specific shipping standard that would be applied to all members of the federation.\n\nParticipation requires a system with at least §H50 Trade§! flowing through it. Furthermore, the existence of an §HOffworld Trading Company§! would mean increased statistical data, and so is encouraged.\n\nDue to the vast scope of this project, some costs will apply. But it's for the greater good. That is to say: more future profit.\n\nEvery member of the federation is strongly encouraged to participate.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:792`

## fedev.605.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.605.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一个绝妙的提案！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

An excellent initiative!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:793`

## fedev.615.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.615.name&lang=en)

### 中文

参与试点—已获准许

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

Trial Participation - Granted

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:795`

## fedev.615.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.615.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

物流重构试点已经启动！多亏了我们帝国兴旺的贸易路线，§Y[From.GetName]§!已获准参与试点项目！一处恒星基地已被挑出，用作试验站点，现在我们需要指派一艘§H工程船§!，以实施实际的重构项目。时间也是一个因素，所以不要再作犹豫。\n\n我们还拥有一处§H外星贸易公司§!设施，正因如此，项目进度有所加快。公司所属的帝国将获得一些金融优待，以表感激。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

The logistical remodelling trial has begun! Thanks to the empire's vigorous trade routes, §Y[From.GetName]§! has been granted participation in the trial project! One starbase has been singled out as a test site, and now a §HConstruction Ship§! must be assigned to perform the actual remodelling project. Time is a factor, so do not linger in this endeavour.\n\nAn §HOffworld Trading Company§! facility is also available, and thanks to this, the project will be expedited. The empire will be granted some financial leniency as a token of gratitude.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:796`

## fedev.615.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.615.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

物流重构试点已经启动！多亏了我们帝国兴旺的贸易路线，§Y[From.GetName]§!已获准参与试点项目！一处恒星基地已被挑出，用作试验站点，现在我们需要指派一艘§H工程船§!，以实施实际的重构项目。时间也是一个因素，所以不要再作犹豫。\n\n我们没有§H外星贸易公司§!。这不会阻止我们参与试点，但会在一定程度上妨碍项目进度，因为我们无法与所涉各方分享有价值的信息，以便充分对其进行利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

The logistical remodelling trial has begun! Thanks to the empire's vigorous trade routes, §Y[From.GetName]§! has been granted participation in the trial project! One starbase has been singled out as a test site, and now a §HConstruction Ship§! must be assigned to perform the actual remodelling project. Time is a factor, so do not linger in this endeavour.\n\nAn §HOffworld Trading Company§! facility was not available. This doesn't stop our participation, but will impede progress somewhat as we cannot take full advantage of beneficial information-sharing between involved parties.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:797`

## fedev.615.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.615.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会做好份内的事！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

We are doing our part!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:798`

## fedev.620.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.620.name&lang=en)

### 中文

参与试点—已被拒绝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

Trial Participation - Denied

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:800`

## fedev.620.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.620.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物流重构试点已经启动！但是，§Y[From.GetName]§!缺少参与试点所必需的设施。一个拥有充足贸易额流经的恒星系居然没有§H贸易中心§!，真是匪夷所思。\n\n错过有潜在好处的项目算是个错误，但连能带来巨大贸易额的顶级商业机会也会错过？这简直是犯罪！\n\n多么耻辱，[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！多么耻辱啊！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

The logistical remodelling trial has begun! However, §Y[From.GetName]§! has been found lacking in facilities necessary for participation in the trial. The lack of a §HTrade Hub§! in a system with sufficient Trade flowing through it is nothing short of baffling.\n\nTo miss out on a potentially beneficial project is a mistake, but to miss out on such a supreme business opportunity as Trade? That's almost a crime!\n\nFor shame, [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! For shame!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:801`

## fedev.625.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.625.name&lang=en)

### 中文

重构完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

Remodelling Complete

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:804`

## fedev.625.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.625.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

恭喜！重构操作已经结束，专用的贸易中心正按照全新的物流标准运行。除了对这一恒星系进行评估之外，没什么需要做的了。\n\n在试验期结束时，关于试点项目结果的官方声明将会发布。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

Congratulations! The remodelling operation has finished and the dedicated Trade Hub is now operating to a new logistical standard. Nothing left to do now but to evaluate the new system.\n\nAt the end of the trial period there will be an official statement on how the trial project turned out.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:805`

## fedev.635.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.635.name&lang=en)

### 中文

财源滚滚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

Smoothest of Rides

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:807`

## fedev.635.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.635.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是就贸易与利润而言意义深远的一天！不仅物流试点成功完成，其结果更是表明星际商业往来更为顺畅，贸易效率显著提升！\n\n结果是，联邦内一切太空贸易操作均会逐步地、不知不觉地切换到新标准进行；整个联邦将由此极大受益。\n\n为利润而欢呼吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

This is a monumental day for profit and trade! Not only is the logistics trial complete, but the results show a noticeable increase in efficiency and smooth interstellar business!\n\nAs a result, all space trading operations in the federation will now progressively - and quite unnoticeably - switch over to the new standard; the federation will benefit tremendously.\n\nHurrah for profit!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:808`

## fedev.635.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.635.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是贸易的胜利！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

A triumph for trade!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:809`

## fedev.640.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.640.name&lang=en)

### 中文

试点成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

Successful Trial

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:811`

## fedev.640.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.640.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是就贸易与商业而言伟大的一天。试验期已经结束，项目已得到完全评估。我们收集到足够的数据表明贸易效率有所提升，应当全面实施新的物流标准。\n\n现在开始，一切运输操作将逐步切换到新标准——但这个过程是完全自动的。食品会被递送，消费品会被送到它们的目的地，香料仍在运输，如此这般。\n\n总之，干得漂亮！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:722`

### English

It's a great day for trade and commerce. The trial period is over, and the project has been fully evaluated. Enough data was collected to see an increase in efficiency that warrants full implementation of the new logistical standard.\n\nNow begins a process in which all shipping operations will switch to the new standard - but this process is fully automatic. The foodstuff will be delivered, the consumer goods will reach their destination, the spice will flow, and so on.\n\nGood job!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:812`

## fedev.640.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.640.a&lang=en)

### 中文

向利润来源问好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

Well hello, revenue stream.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:813`

## fedev.645.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.645.name&lang=en)

### 中文

试点失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

Pathetic Participation

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:815`

## fedev.645.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.645.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果联邦各成员国逐利的动机微弱到这种程度，那灰暗的时代确实来临了。\n\n由于缺乏参与及动力到了令人费解的程度，这项大有前途的物流试点变成了一场闹剧！如今还剩的只有冗长乏味的指责、官司还有诉讼了。\n\n去请个律师吧，[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]——会有些不良后果需要处理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

Grim times do indeed loom if this is the substandard motivation by which the federation's members pursue profit.\n\nA baffling lack of participation and drive turned this promising logistical trial into nothing short of a farce! Now the only thing that remains is a tedious dance of blame, lawsuits and litigation.\n\nLawyer up, [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural] - there will be repercussions.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:816`

## fedev.645.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afedev.645.a&lang=en)

这可有点尴尬……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

This is somewhat embarrassing...

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:817`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALOGISTIC_REMODEL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

为了对全新的物流标准进行试点，这一恒星基地的相关设施需要进行重大的结构性改造。\n\n指派一艘§Y工程船§!到这个项目，确保项目按时完成！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

In order to try out the new logistical standards, the relevant facilities on this starbase need to undergo some major structural alterations.\n\nAssign a §YConstruction Ship§! to the project, and make sure it's done on time!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:831`

## LOGISTIC_REMODEL_OFFWORLD

### English

## LOGISTIC_REMODEL_OFFWORLD_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALOGISTIC_REMODEL_OFFWORLD_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

为了对全新的物流标准进行试点，这一恒星基地的相关设施需要进行重大的结构性改造。多亏了本地的§H外星贸易公司§!，项目会进行的更为高效。\n\n指派一艘§Y工程船§!到这个项目，确保项目按时完成！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

In order to try out the new logistical standards, the relevant facilities on this starbase need to undergo some major structural alterations. Thanks to the local §HOffworld Trading Company§! this project will be more efficient.\n\nAssign a §YConstruction Ship§! to the project, and make sure it's done on time!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:833`

## federations2.50.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.50.name&lang=en)

### 中文

联邦团结暨领导力盛典

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

A Celebration of Unity and Leadership

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:837`

## federations2.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]在我国的明智领导下繁荣昌盛。许多[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]如今感到是时候因我们为盟友提供的服务获得回报了，我们将举办一场团结盛典——更确切地说，是为向联邦无私奉献的伟大[Root.GetAdj]领袖们举办的盛典。他们提议各成员国合作在[Root.Capital.GetName]的天空中树立一道丰碑，于此同时，我们将派遣代表团前往两个成员国的首都，协助他们为[Root.Federation.GetName]带来更多的凝聚力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

The [Root.Federation.GetName] has prospered under the wise leadership of our nation. Many [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] now feel that it is time for our services to the alliance to be paid back with a celebration of its unity - and more specifically, the great [Root.GetAdj] leaders that have done so much for it. They propose that member states contribute towards the erection of a grand monument in the skies above [Root.Capital.GetName], while in the meantime we send delegations to two member states' capitals to aid them in their efforts to bring greater unity to the [Root.Federation.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:838`

## federations2.50.A.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.50.A.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

所有能前往§H[Root.Capital.System.GetName]§!星系的成员国均会被邀请参与联合行动。\n\n如果他们接受，他们必须投入资源建造纪念碑，这会增加联邦§H经验§!和[Root.GetName]的§H影响力§!。\n\n[Root.GetName]还可以选择在两个成员国中提升§H[Root.GetHegemonyEthos]§!的吸引力。\n\n如果这一提议遭到广泛拒绝，联邦§H团结度§!将会下降，[Root.GetName]会损失§H影响力§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

All member states with access to the §H[Root.Capital.System.GetName]§! system will be approached to take part in the joint operation.\n\nIf they accept, they will have to dedicate resources to a monument which will boost Federation §HExperience§! and the [Root.GetName]'s §HInfluence§!.\n\nThe [Root.GetName] will also be able to choose to increase §H[Root.GetHegemonyEthos]§! attraction in two member states.\n\nIf the proposal is widely rejected, Federation §HCohesion§! will decrease and the [Root.GetName] will lose §HInfluence§!.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:839`

## federations2.50.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.50.A&lang=en)

### 中文

听起来是个好主意！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

Sounds like a great idea!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:840`

## federations2.50.B

没必要折腾这种琐事。

### English

We have no need for such trinkets.

## federations2.51.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.51.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$federations2.50.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

$federations2.50.name$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:842`

## federations2.51.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.51.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们好，尊贵的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePluralCompliment]！在我们明智而宽宥的领导下，[Root.Federation.GetName]的实力与日俱增，我们共同目标遍及星海。是时候庆祝我们联合起来取得的成就了。还有什么会比各成员国合力在我国首都树立一座丰碑更合宜呢？与此同时，我国会派遣代表团前往贵国首都，协助你们求取[Root.Federation.GetName]的上上善道。\n\n你们愿意加入这次活动吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

Greetings, noble [Root.GetSpeciesNamePluralCompliment]! Under our wise and magnanimous leadership, the [Root.Federation.GetName] has gone from strength to strength, projecting our unity of purpose across the galaxy. The time has come to celebrate our joint successes as a union. What better way to do this than through a grand monument over our capital, erected through the efforts of the member states? In the meantime, we will send a delegation to your capital, which will aid your efforts to strive for the greater good of the [Root.Federation.GetName].\n\nWill you join this venture?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:843`

## federations2.51.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.51.A&lang=en)

### 中文

当然愿意！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Of course!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:844`

## federations2.51.A.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.51.A.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

所有能前往§H[From.Capital.System.GetName]§!星系的成员国均会被邀请参与联合行动。\n\n如果他们接受，他们必须投入资源建造纪念碑，这会增加联邦§H经验§!和[From.GetName]的§H影响力§!。\n\n[From.GetName]还可以选择在两个成员国中提升§H[From.GetHegemonyEthos]§!的吸引力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

All member states with access to the §H[From.Capital.System.GetName]§! system will be approached to take part in the joint operation.\n\nIf they accept, they will have to dedicate resources to a monument which will boost Federation §HExperience§! and the [From.GetName]'s §HInfluence§!.\n\nThe [From.GetName] will also be able to choose to increase §H[From.GetHegemonyEthos]§! attraction in two member states.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:845`

## federations2.51.A.tooltip2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.51.A.tooltip2&lang=en)

### 中文

\n完成纪念碑将花费[Root.GetName]£minerals£矿物年产量的10%。

## federations2.53.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.53.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我国大使就我国的团结盛典计划与[Root.Federation.GetName]各成员国进行了商洽。不幸的是，他们对这件事的看法似乎与我们不同，我们没能获得计划推进所必需的支持。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

Our ambassadors have approached the members of the [Root.Federation.GetName] with our plans for a celebration of unity. Unfortunately, it seems that they do not feel the same way about the matter as we do, and we have been unable to muster sufficient support for the plan to proceed.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:852`

## federations2.54.desc.said_yes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.54.desc.said_yes&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管有我们为[From.GetAdj]举办[Root.Federation.GetName]团结盛典的提议背书，这项计划似乎还是失败了。失败原因是其他成员国不愿提供足够支持。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

Despite our backing for the [From.GetAdj] initiative to hold a celebration of the [Root.Federation.GetName]'s unity, it seems that the plan has fallen through. There was simply not enough support for it among the other member states.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:862`

## federations2.54.desc.said_no

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.54.desc.said_no&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[Root.Federation.GetName]中[From.GetAdj]专横的联合行动计划的反对有了成效。震惊于这项计划打算在成员国间播撒[From.GetAdj]宣传，这项提议被各方广泛回绝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

Our opposition to the overbearing [From.GetAdj] plans for a joint operation in the [Root.Federation.GetName] has borne fruit. Appalled at the plans for missions to spread [From.GetAdj] propaganda among the member states, the proposals were roundly rejected by all parties.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:863`

## federations2.55.name

### English

Joint Operation Approved

## federations2.55.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.55.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们关于举办一场联合盛典，庆祝[Root.Federation.GetName]的力量与团结的计划已获大多数成员国认可。我们将立刻开始致力于实施这项计划，增进联盟内的和谐。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

Our initiative to hold a joint celebration of the strength and unity of the [Root.Federation.GetName] has met with approval among the majority of members. We will begin work on implementing our plans to spread harmony within the alliance immediately!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:867`

## federations2.55.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.55.A&lang=en)

### 中文

为了力量与团结！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:776`

### English

For strength and unity!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:868`

## hegemon_join_operation_happens_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemon_join_operation_happens_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

所有参与的成员国获得在§H[federation_leader.Capital.GetName]§!上建造纪念碑的特殊项目。\n§H[federation_leader.GetName]§!获得特殊项目，可以向至多两个成员国传播§H[federation_leader.GetHegemonyEthos]§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

### English

All participating members gain a special project to build a monument on §H[federation_leader.Capital.GetName]§!.\nThe §H[federation_leader.GetName]§! gains special projects to spread the §H[federation_leader.GetHegemonyEthos]§! ethos to up to two member states.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:869`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:870`

## federations2.56.desc.said_yes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.56.desc.said_yes&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetAdj]关于举办一场联合盛典，庆祝[Root.Federation.GetName]的力量与团结的计划已获大多数成员国认可。我们将立即开始工作，以便计划早日落实！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

The [From.GetAdj] initiative to hold a joint celebration of the strength and unity of the [Root.Federation.GetName] has met with approval among the majority of members. We will begin work to make the plans a reality immediately!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:871`

## federations2.56.desc.said_no

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.56.desc.said_no&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我国反对，[Root.Federation.GetName]中[From.GetAdj]专横的联合行动计划得到了大多数成员国的认可。幸好，[From.GetName]无法强迫我国参与这项计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

Despite our opposition, overbearing [From.GetAdj] plans for a joint operation in the [Root.Federation.GetName] have met with approval among the majority of members. Fortunately, there is nothing the [From.GetName] can do to force us to participate in the scheme.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:872`

## federations2.56.desc.invalid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.56.desc.invalid&lang=en)

### 中文

为了向[From.GetName]在[Root.Federation.GetName]中提供的强力领导致以敬意，[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]已经得到成员国认可，举行一场关于联盟团结的联合庆典。成员国同意投入资源，在[From.Capital.GetName]的天空中树立一座丰碑，同时[From.GetAdj]代表团将访问若干国家的首都，协助成员国融入联盟。\n\n由于我国位置偏远，很难前往[From.Capital.GetName]，我们无法参与这一计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

In honor of the strong leadership offered by the [From.GetName] in the [Root.Federation.GetName], the [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have secured approval among its members for a joint celebration of our alliance's unity. Member states have agreed to dedicate resources to a grand monument in the skies above [From.Capital.GetName], while [From.GetAdj] delegations will visit various capitals to aid the integration of the member states into the alliance.\n\nGiven our remote location, with no easy access to [From.Capital.GetName], we are unable to participate in the scheme.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:873`

## hegemony_joint_project_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_joint_project_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一项联合行动已经开始，[Root.Federation.GetName]成员国将以此庆祝其强有力的领导并推动意识形态上的团结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

A joint operation has been launched between members of the [Root.Federation.GetName] to celebrate its strong leadership and push for greater ideological unity.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:877`

## hegemony_joint_project_leader

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_joint_project_leader&lang=en)

### 中文

对[This.GetName]的文化调整

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

Cultural Adjustment on [This.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:878`

## hegemony_joint_project_leader_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_joint_project_leader_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

作为当前庆祝[Root.Owner.Federation.GetName]强力领导暨意识形态团结的联合行动的一部分，代表团将被派往两个成员国的首都，以监督其更紧密地融入联盟。\n\n一旦两个成员国已被选定，文化调整项目将被视作完成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

As part of the current joint operation to celebrate the strong leadership and ideological unity of the [Root.Owner.Federation.GetName], a delegation shall be sent to two members' capitals in order to supervise their closer integration into the alliance.\n\nOnce the two member states have been chosen, the cultural adjustment program will be deemed complete.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:879`

## hegemony_joint_project_member

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_joint_project_member&lang=en)

### 中文

强力领导纪念碑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

A Monument to Strong Leadership

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:880`

## hegemony_joint_project_member_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_joint_project_member_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

为了庆贺[Root.Owner.Federation.GetName]的凝聚力和实力，我们目前正在执行联合行动。作为行动的一部分，我们已经同意向[This.GetName]运送资源，以建造献给[This.Owner.GetAdj]领袖们的丰碑。\n\n我们将为此项目献出£minerals£矿物年产量的10%。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

As part of the current joint operation to celebrate the unity and strength of the [Root.Owner.Federation.GetName], we have agreed to send resources to [This.GetName] for the construction of a grand monument to our [This.Owner.GetAdj] leaders.\n\nWe will have to contribute 10% of our £minerals£ mineral output for a year to this project.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:881`

## federations2.57.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.57.name&lang=en)

### English

## federations2.57.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.57.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派去[FromFrom.Owner.GetName]宣扬[Root.GetHegemonyEthos]理想的代表团已于[FromFrom.GetName]落脚。幸运的话，他们的努力会有回报，[Root.Federation.GetName]内将更为和谐。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

Our delegation to spread [Root.GetHegemonyEthos] ideals in the [FromFrom.Owner.GetName] has established itself on [FromFrom.GetName]. With luck, their efforts will bear fruit and increase harmony within the [Root.Federation.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:883`

## federations2.58.name

### English

[From.Owner.GetAdj] Delegation Established on [FromFrom.GetName]

## federations2.58.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.58.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为当前联邦联合行动的一部分，[From.Owner.GetAdj][From.Owner.GetGovernmentName]已经派出代表团前往[FromFrom.GetName]，以在[Root.GetName]宣扬[From.Owner.GetHegemonyEthos]理想。尽管他们宣称这是为了在[Root.Federation.GetName]内增进和谐与善意，许多人担忧我们自己的价值观会因我们在联邦中的成员国资格受损。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

As part of the current federation joint operations, the [From.Owner.GetAdj] [From.Owner.GetGovernmentName] has sent delegates to [FromFrom.GetName] to spread [From.Owner.GetHegemonyEthos] ideals in the [Root.GetName]. Although they claim to be promoting harmony and goodwill within the [Root.Federation.GetName], many are concerned that our own values are being compromised by our membership in the alliance.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:885`

## federations2.58.desc.good

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.58.desc.good&lang=en)

### 中文

作为当前联邦联合行动的一部分，[From.Owner.GetAdj][From.Owner.GetGovernmentName]已经派出代表团前往[FromFrom.GetName]，以在[Root.GetName]宣扬[From.Owner.GetHegemonyEthos]理想。幸好，他们的价值观念与我们一致，相信这一定会增进[Root.Federation.GetName]内的和谐与善意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

As part of the current federation joint operations, the [From.Owner.GetAdj] [From.Owner.GetGovernmentName] has sent delegates to [FromFrom.GetName] to spread [From.Owner.GetHegemonyEthos] ideals in the [Root.GetName]. Fortunately, their values align well with our own, lending credence to their claim to be promoting harmony and goodwill within the [Root.Federation.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:886`

## federations2.59.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.59.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完成了就[FromFrom.GetName]纪念碑的工作，以庆祝[Root.Federation.GetName]的团结及[FromFrom.Owner.GetName]提供的开明领导。他们对我们的付出表示感谢，但在纪念碑正式揭幕前尚需等待我们的联邦盟友完成贡献。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

We have completed our work on the monument over [FromFrom.GetName], celebrating the unity of the [Root.Federation.GetName] and the enlightened leadership offered by the [FromFrom.Owner.GetName]. They thank us for our offering, although they await the contributions of our federation allies before the monument is officially unveiled.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:890`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

Once all members have completed their contributions, the Federation will gain an experience boost.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:891`

## hegemony_federation_monument_complete_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_federation_monument_complete_success&lang=en)

### 中文

揭幕式上锣鼓喧天，各成员国的星球上一片沸腾，我们纪念碑的华丽光彩清晰地映入眼帘。成员国间奋勇争先，没有任何偷工减料。最终的成果——原本献给[FromFrom.Owner.GetAdj][FromFrom.Owner.GetRulerTitle]——如今向所有人证明了我们联邦的团结与力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

It has been unveiled to much fanfare, sparking celebrations across all member worlds as the splendor of our creation became clear. Each member competed with all others to outdo them in their contribution, with none slacking off. The work - originally dedicated to the [FromFrom.Owner.GetAdj] [FromFrom.Owner.GetRulerTitle] - now stands as evidence for all to see, of the strength and togetherness of our federation.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:894`

## hegemony_federation_monument_partial_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_federation_monument_partial_success&lang=en)

### 中文

揭幕式上锣鼓喧天，各成员国的星球上一片沸腾，我们纪念碑的华丽光彩清晰地映入眼帘。几乎所有成员国都投入了可观的努力，最终成果是我们联盟力量与团结的典范。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

It has been unveiled to much fanfare, sparking celebrations across member worlds as the splendor of our creation became clear. Nearly all member states gave significant contributions, making the work a fine example of our union's strength and togetherness.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:895`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:896`

## federations2.60.desc.partial_success

### 中文

## federations2.60.desc.partial_failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.60.desc.partial_failure&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完成了就[FromFrom.GetName]纪念碑的工作，以庆祝[Root.Federation.GetName]的团结和[FromFrom.Owner.GetName]提供的开明领导。不过，我们的盟友似乎不如我们这般支持这项提案，大多数成员国除了空话以外再无贡献。最终的纪念碑了无趣味——与其说是反映了联盟的共同感和力量，不如说是尴尬地象征着同盟间的分歧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

We have completed our work on the monument over [FromFrom.GetName] to celebrate the unity of the [Root.Federation.GetName] and the enlightened leadership offered by the [FromFrom.Owner.GetName]. However, it seems that our allies did not support the initiative as strongly as we did, and a majority of federation members did not contribute much beyond empty words. The resulting monument is therefore rather lackluster - an embarrassment more symbolic of our alliance's divisions than any common feeling or strength.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:898`

## federations2.61.desc.partial_failure.haha_no

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.61.desc.partial_failure.haha_no&lang=en)

### 中文

我国盟友在[FromFrom.GetName]建造纪念碑，以庆祝[Root.Federation.GetName]的“团结”和[FromFrom.Owner.GetName]提供的“开明领导”的工作已经完成。不过，对这项提案的支持不如[FromFrom.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]希望的那样有力，大多数成员国除了空话以外再无贡献。最终的纪念碑了无趣味——与其说是反映了联盟的共同感和力量，不如说是尴尬地象征着同盟间的分歧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:812`

### English

Our allies have finished their work on the monument over [FromFrom.GetName] to celebrate the "unity" of the [Root.Federation.GetName] and the "enlightened leadership" offered by the [FromFrom.Owner.GetName]. It seems that commitment to the initiative was not as strong as the [FromFrom.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] had hoped, however, and a majority of federation members did not contribute much beyond empty words. The resulting monument is therefore rather lackluster - an embarrassment more symbolic of our alliance's divisions than any common feeling or strength.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:904`

## federations2.61.desc.complete_failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.61.desc.complete_failure&lang=en)

### 中文

由[FromFrom.Owner.GetName]提议，在[FromFrom.GetName]建造纪念碑，以庆祝[Root.Federation.GetName]的“团结”和“开明领导”的计划今天已被正式取消。$hegemony_federation_monument_complete_failure$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

Plans for a monument over [FromFrom.GetName] to celebrate the "unity" of the [Root.Federation.GetName] and the "enlightened leadership" offered by the [FromFrom.Owner.GetName] have officially been cancelled today. $hegemony_federation_monument_complete_failure$

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:905`

## hegemony_federation_monument_complete_failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahegemony_federation_monument_complete_failure&lang=en)

### 中文

同盟中没有成员国愿意为计划做除了空话之外的贡献，也就是说这个纪念碑只算是又一个不切实际的构想——一个失败的点子，表现不出任何共同感或是力量，反而象征着我们之间的分歧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

Not one member of the alliance committed anything but hollow words to the initiative, meaning that the monument will go down as just another unrealized scheme - a failed idea symbolic more of our divisions than any common feeling or strength.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:906`

## federations2.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

联邦舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

The Federation Fleet

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:954`

## federations2.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]代表星系舞台上一股强大的力量，但是其舰队分散在各处，并且在不同的组织结构下运作。近日，联邦各地几位颇具影响力的军事专家对我们能否面对协调统一、实力强大的敌人表示担忧。\n\n为弥补实力上的不足，他们制定了一套方案，计划在联邦范围内进行一系列军事演习，以便建立军队凝聚力，磨练船员的技能。根据他们的计划，每支舰队将派出特遣队前往盟友领土的特定地点，进行一系列复杂的演习，与其他参加的舰队相互协调，确保行动同步进行。成功完成演习是我军的重要使命，将大大提高我们联邦的战备水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

The [Root.Federation.GetName] represents a powerful force on the galactic stage; however, its fleets are divided and operate under a disparate set of organizational structures. Recently, several influential military experts from across the federation have raised concerns about our ability to face a well-coordinated and powerful enemy.\n\nTo rectify this, they have drawn up plans for a federation-wide series of military exercises to build cohesion within our militaries and hone the skills of our crews. Under their plans, detachments from each of our fleets would move to specific locations in our allies' territories and perform a series of complex maneuvers, coordinating with other participating fleets to ensure that the operations are executed simultaneously. Successfully holding these exercises would be a significant commitment for our armed forces but would appreciably boost our federation's military readiness.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:955`

## federations2.100.A.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.100.A.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将与所有成员国联系，要求他们参加演习。\n\n如果大量成员国拒绝此提议，联邦§H团结度§!将会降低，而[Root.GetName]将损失§H影响力§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

All member states will be approached and asked to participate in the exercises.\n\nIf the proposal is widely rejected, Federation §HCohesion§! will decrease and the [Root.GetName] will lose §HInfluence§!.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:956`

## federations2.101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊贵的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePluralCompliment]，你们好！我们齐心协力，打造了一股强大的力量，即使在星系舞台上也不容小觑。但是，我们担心，如果出现实力强大、协调统一的威胁，我们目前的实力可能不足以与之抗衡。我们的专家制定了一套方案，计划进行一系列联邦联合军事演习，以便建立军队凝聚力，磨练船员的技能：我们打算让每支舰队派出特遣队前往联邦领土内的特定地点，进行一系列复杂的演习，与其他参加的舰队相互协调，确保行动同步进行。\n\n我们相信，这些演习将大大提高我们联邦的战备水平。你们愿意参与演习吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

### English

Greetings, noble [Root.GetSpeciesNamePluralCompliment]! Together, we have built a powerful force worthy of notice on the galactic stage. However, we are concerned that this may not be enough should a powerful and well-coordinated threat arise. Our experts have drawn up plans for a series of joint federation exercises to build cohesion within our militaries and hone the skills of our crews: we intend that detachments from each of our fleets move to specific locations in federation territory and perform a series of complex maneuvers, coordinating with other participating fleets to ensure that the operations are executed simultaneously.\n\nWe are confident that these exercises would appreciably boost our federation's military readiness. Will you join this venture?

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:960`

## federations2.103.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.103.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的大使已竭尽全力说服[Root.Federation.GetName]成员国参与我们的联合军事演习计划。但遗憾的是，我们的盟友普遍不感兴趣，比起提高联合作战能力，他们更愿意相信自己的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

Our ambassadors have sought to persuade the members of the [Root.Federation.GetName] to participate in our plans for joint military exercises. Unfortunately, however, they have been met with widespread disinterest, our allies preferring to trust in their own strength rather than seeking to improve our joint capacities.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:968`

### English

## federations2.104.desc.said_yes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.104.desc.said_yes&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们支持举行联邦联合军事演习的[From.GetAdj]提议，但由于其他成员国并未对此提供足够的支持，计划似乎已经宣告失败。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:876`

### English

Despite our backing for the [From.GetAdj] initiative to hold joint federation military exercises, it seems that the plan has fallen through. There was simply not enough support for it among the other member states.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:971`

## federations2.104.desc.said_no

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:877`

### English

Our opposition to [From.GetAdj] plans for joint military exercises in the [Root.Federation.GetName] has borne fruit. A majority of members saw no need to waste our military's attention on such trivialities.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:972`

## federations2.106.desc.said_no

## federations2.109.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.109.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

所有联邦舰队现已就位，训练演习可立即开始！一组高机动性的轻装目标从先前隐藏的位置现身，在为期30天的演习中，这些目标会尽力避免被我们的舰船摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

All federation fleets are now in place, so the training exercises may commence! A set of lightly-armed but highly mobile targets have emerged from a previously hidden location and are seeking to avoid destruction by our ships before the 30 days time limit expires.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:991`

### English

## federations2.112.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.112.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已成功消灭联合军事演习中的目标。只能希望我们的盟友也同样勤奋！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

## federations2.113.name

### English
