## federations2.113.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.113.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们未能消灭联合军事演习中的目标，舰队军官因此羞愧难当。我们只能希望盟友能够更加成功！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:901`

### English

To the immeasurable shame of our naval officers, we have failed to eliminate our target in the joint military exercises. We can only hope that our allies have been more successful!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:996`

## federations2.115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

联合军事演习完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:902`

### English

Joint Military Exercises Complete

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:997`

## federations2.115.desc.complete_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.115.desc.complete_success&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]的联合军事演习现已结束。这次演习称得上是鼓舞人心的巨大成功，每支参与演习的队伍都不费吹灰之力地摧毁了目标，彻底证明了我们联邦舰队超凡的技能和卓越的协调能力！在向我们开战前，敌人一定会再三考虑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:903`

### English

The [Root.Federation.GetName]'s joint military exercises have now concluded. They have immediately been declared a rousing success, each participating fleet having struck down their targets without untoward difficulty, proving once and for all the excellent skill and superior coordination of our federation fleet! Our enemies will surely think twice before they pick a fight with us now.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:998`

## federations2.115.desc.partial_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.115.desc.partial_success&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]的联合军事演习现已结束。这次演习相对来说差强人意：尽管并非所有舰队都能消灭其目标，但大部分参加的舰队都取得了成功，这说明联邦舰队有能力协调执行复杂的任务。此外，我们的船员也从这次经历中受益颇多，目前像往常一样准备充分，随时可以执行任务。在向我们开战前，敌人一定会再三考虑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

The [Root.Federation.GetName]'s joint military exercises have now concluded. They have been declared a partial success: although not every party was able to eliminate their target, most participating fleets were able to do so, demonstrating that the federation's fleet is capable of coordinating to perform sophisticated tasks. Additionally, our crews have benefited greatly from the experience and are now as ready for action as ever before. Our enemies will surely think twice before they pick a fight with us now.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:999`

## federations2.115.desc.partial_failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.115.desc.partial_failure&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]的军事演习现已结束，但过程有一点讽刺：尽管部分参加的舰队能够击中目标，但大多数舰队都未能成功，这让我们不禁怀疑，[Root.Federation.GetName]是否有能力组织统一的军事行动。我们只能希望敌人没有过于密切地关注演习的发展……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

The [Root.Federation.GetName]'s joint military exercises have ended in a slightly farcical manner: although some participants were able to strike their targets, a majority failed to do so, casting doubt on the [Root.Federation.GetName]'s ability to work as a unified force in military matters.  We can only hope that our enemies were not watching proceedings too closely...

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1000`

## federations2.115.desc.complete_failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations2.115.desc.complete_failure&lang=en)

### 中文

居然没有一个参加演习的成员国能在时限内消灭目标，[Root.Federation.GetName]的联合军事演习也随之以闹剧告终。我们只能希望，在我们尽力捡起失去的骄傲、恢复舰队的尊严时，敌人没有过于密切地关注演习的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

With the failure of even one participating member to eliminate their target before time ran out, the [Root.Federation.GetName]'s joint military exercises have ended in farce. We can only hope that our enemies were not watching proceedings too closely as we seek to pick up the pieces of our lost pride and restore the dignity of our fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1001`

## federations3.2205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

联邦主教会议

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:939`

### English

Federation Ecumenical Council

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1037`

## federations3.2205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Federation.GetName]的成员们以圣灵之名汇聚一堂。我们在各自的信仰之间找到了共同点，但联邦内仍然还有着多种信条、宗教和教会，和谐共处并不容易。\n\n最近，僧团的成员敦促[Root.Federation.GetName]主持一场主教会议，以便共商起草一份联合教条，定义我们信仰的根基。这个提案被通过了，每一个[Root.Federation.GetName]的成员都要派出他们的代表。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

The members of the [Root.Federation.GetName] are united around their spirituality. We found common ground in the fact that we are believers, but the federation is home to various creeds, faiths and churches and cohabitation is sometimes uneasy.\n\nLately, members of the clergies have been pushing for the [Root.Federation.GetName] to hold an Ecumenical Council, where we could gather to try and outline a unified dogma, and define the core tenets of our beliefs. The idea was approved, and every member of the [Root.Federation.GetName] has been asked to send representatives. 

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1038`

## federations3.2215.name

### 中文

主教会议：无瑕理论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:945`

### English

Ecumenical Council: Flawless Theology

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1043`

## federations3.2215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天的议题是“[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]”，我们的使团用最华丽的辞藻和无可辩驳的逻辑作出了一场令人印象深刻的陈辞，从气势上彻底碾压了那些反对者我们观点的声音。得益于我们的精彩表现，对手陷入了沉默并且会议很快得出结论。各国使团希望能因我们的智慧而给予嘉奖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:946`

### English

During today's debates about "[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]", our delegation made a brilliant display of rhetoric and implacable logic, virtually crushing those arguing against our point of view. With the opposition silenced, a conclusion was swiftly reached thanks to us. Other delegations wish to honor us for our wisdom.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1044`

## federations3.2215.a

### English

### English

As if things could have gone any other way!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1047`

## federations3.2220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

主教会议：辩论白热化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:950`

### English

Ecumenical Council: Heated Debate

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1048`

## federations3.2220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

关于“[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]”这个问题相当地微妙，相关的议论刺激着许多敏感的神经。具体来说，我们的使团非常固执己见，并且对各国的高级教士屡屡出言不逊。他们纷纷要求我们正式致歉。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:951`

### English

The question of "[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]" is a delicate one and many sensitivities where hurt in the discussions surrounding it. In particular, our delegation was most headstrong and has been recorded insulting several other high ranking prelates from other empires. They have requested an official apology from us. 

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1049`

## federations3.2220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这种举止简直丢人。我们道歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:952`

### English

Actions like these are not acceptable. Apologize.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1050`

### English

## federations3.2225.name

### English

Ecumenical Council: A Matter of Precedence

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1053`

## federations3.2225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在讨论“[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]”这个话题之前，议会要求使团们在一位高阶教士的领导下进行平静的冥想。由于议程制定不详细，有三位显赫的主教都认为他们自己才应当主持这场仪式。氛围变得焦灼了起来，随着他们争吵的升级，会议不得不延期举行。  

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:956`

### English

Before discussing the topic of "[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]", the delegations were supposed to be led in peaceful meditation by a high ranking prelate. Due to imprecisions in the schedule, three prominent priests thought that they would be the ones to lead the ceremony. The situation escalated and their bickerings have brought the council to a halt.  

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1054`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

关于“[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]”的激烈讨论让每一位与会嘉宾都备受刺激情绪激动。为了缓和气氛，会议决定让每一个国家都互相交换礼物以及友谊的象征。我们要送些什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:961`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

主教会议：危险联络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:978`

### English

Ecumenical Council: Dangerous Liaisons

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1076`

## federations3.2240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今日关于“[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]”的关键辩论被一位突然闯入的高级教士打断了，他拖着两位不同使团的成员。这两人被撞见时正在述说着对彼此跨越种族的恒久爱恋。较为保守的特使们认为他们的行为很不体面而且已经接近异端行为，有辱这场会议的神圣性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:979`

### English

The day's crucial debate about "[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]" was suddenly interrupted when a prelate came in, dragging along two members of different delegations. They were caught professing their undying love to each other, despite their differences. The more conservative envoys view their behavior as disrespectful and borderline heretical, violating the sanctity of this holy congress.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1077`

## federations3.2240.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2240.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这明明是团结的象征！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:980`

### English

They are a symbol of unity!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1078`

### English

### English

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1080`

## federations3.2245.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2245.name&lang=en)

### 中文

主教会议：宴会丑闻

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:983`

### English

Ecumenical Council: Scandalous Feast

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1081`

## federations3.2245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在激烈争论“[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]”一整天之后，按照其中一个使团的习俗举行了宴会。宴会的一个环节是向各位嘉宾呈上仪式性的酒水。对于那个使团的物种而言这是一种很弱的致幻剂，但对我们而言则反应强烈。仅过了一小会儿整个房间就陷入了混乱的斗殴中。所幸事态平息后没有人受到严重的伤害，但调查认为是我们的特使最先动的手，要求我们对此道歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:984`

### English

After a long day spent discussing "[GetEcumenicalCouncilDebate]", a feast was held following the custom of one of the delegations. As part of it, a ritual draught was served to everyone. To them, it is a mild inebriant, but our delegation had an extreme reaction to it, and moments later, the room erupted in a general brawl. Things have settled down and no major injuries have been reported, but we are regarded as having started the fight, and are expected to issue an apology.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1082`

## federations3.2245.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2245.a&lang=en)

### 中文

道歉吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:985`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1083`

### English

Blame the drinks!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1084`

## federations3.2245.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2245.c&lang=en)

### 中文

行，把他们全都送到一颗死寂星球静养去吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:987`

### English

Send everyone on a meditative retreat on a tomb world.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1085`

## federations3.2255.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2255.name&lang=en)

### 中文

主教会议：各执己见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:988`

### English

Ecumenical Council: Discord Prevails

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1086`

## federations3.2255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在下决心主持会议时我们就知道，通向联合教条的路必定长满荆棘，但我们还是低谷了荆棘到底长得有多满。几个月毫无意义的争吵没有得出任何结论，反而是让我们更加意识到互相之间的不可调和。我们决定等过一段时间再提这事了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:989`

### English

When we decided to hold the council, we knew the road to a unified dogma would be long and hard, but we underestimated just how long and how hard it would be. Months of sterile debates have done nothing but highlight everything that separates us from each other. We have decided to wait a while before trying again.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1087`

## federations3.2260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

主教会议：求同存异

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:990`

### English

Ecumenical Council: Concord Prevails

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1088`

## federations3.2260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们确是受到眷顾的。最后发现，我们一开始忧心忡忡的事情不过是杞人忧天，一切进行得都无比丝滑。精准的理论和发人深省的演讲使我们成功地编撰出了和谐的新教条文集。我们很快会再重新修缮一回，但现在它已经成为了联合信仰的基础，拉近了彼此之间的距离！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:991`

### English

Truly, we are blessed. Whatever apprehensions we had going in have been proven wrong and everything went quite smoothly. Precise theology and inspired discourse have allowed us to create a harmonious corpus of our new dogma. We will surely revisit it later, but for now, it will serve as the foundation of a unified faith that will bring us closer to each other!

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1089`

## federations3.2260.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2260.tt&lang=en)

§G+20%§!岗位产出的£unity£凝聚力以及§G+3§!月度联邦经验值，维持20年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:992`

### English

For 20 years, §G+20%§! £unity£ Unity from jobs and §G+3§! Monthly Federation XP

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1090`

## federations3.2265.name

### 中文

主教会议：初步胜利

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:993`

### English

Ecumenical Council: Tentative Success

## federations3.2265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afederations3.2265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

总的来说，会议还是有所收获的。虽然不是完全顺利，但大家也都做好了面对困难的准备。这只是让我们的人民和信仰走向团结的第一步。道阻且长，但前途光明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/federations_anniversary_l_simp_chinese.yml:994`

### English

Overall, the council was productive. Not everything went smoothly, but difficulties were to be expected. This is only the first step towards unifying our people and our faiths. The road will be long, but what expects us at the end will make it worthwhile.

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1092`

## habitat_central_complex_DESC

### 中文

§Y$habitat_complex$§!的核心轨道站，可以容纳若干£pop£§H人口§!并在非宜居行星的轨道上为£building£§Y建筑§!和£district£§Y区划§!提供空间。

Source: `localisation/english/federations_anniversary_l_english.yml:1172`
